[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:2:7)


**נִסּוּךְ הַמַּיִם.** בְּחַג הַסֻּכּוֹת:


ניסוך המים – on the holiday of Sukkot (see Tractate Sukkah, Chapter 4, Mishnah 9).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:2:8)


**וְהַיַּיִן.** בְּכָל יוֹם. שֶׁשָּׁם הָיוּ הַשִּׁיתִין וְלֹא הָיָה אֶפְשָׁר לְנַסֵּךְ כִּי אִם שָׁם:


והיין – on each day, for there were the pits by the side of the altar into which the remainder of the libations were poured (see also Tractate Meilah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 3 and Talmud Meilah 11a) and it was not possible to offer libations other than there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:2:9)


**וְעוֹלַת הָעוֹף כְּשֶׁהִיא רַבָּה בַמִּזְרָח.** דְּעִקַּר מְקוֹמָהּ בְּקֶרֶן דְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית מִפְּנֵי שֶׁקְּרוֹבָה לְבֵית הַדֶּשֶׁן לִזְרֹק שָׁם מֻרְאָה וְנוֹצָה, כְּדִכְתִיב (שם א) וְהִשְׁלִיךְ אֹתָהּ וְגוֹ'‏ אֶל מְקוֹם הַדָּשֶׁן. וּכְשֶׁהִיא רַבָּה שָׁם שֶׁיֵּשׁ כֹּהֲנִים הַרְבֵּה בְּאוֹתָהּ הַקֶּרֶן עֲסוּקִים בְּעוֹלוֹת וְאֵין לָזֶה מָקוֹם לַעֲמֹד בַּסּוֹבֵב דְּעוֹלַת הָעוֹף נַעֲשֵׂית לְמַעְלָה, בָּא לוֹ לְקֶרֶן דְּרוֹמִית מַעֲרָבִית, שֶׁאַף הִיא סְמוּכָה לְבֵית הַדֶּשֶׁן יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר שְׁתֵּי קְרָנוֹת, שֶׁבֵּית הַדֶּשֶׁן סָמוּךְ לַכֶּבֶשׁ הָיָה בְּמִזְרָחוֹ שֶׁל כֶּבֶשׁ וְלִדְרוֹמוֹ שֶׁל מִזְבֵּחַ:


ועולת העוף כשהיא רבה במזרח – for he essence of its place was in the southeastern corner, because it was close to the place of the ashes to throw there its crop (of the bird) and its feathers, as it is written (Leviticus 1:16): “[He shall remove its crop with its contents,[ and cast it into the place of the ashes, [at the east side of the altar].” But when it is abundant there, that there are many Kohanim in that corner engaged with burnt-offerings, but there isn’t space for this to stand on the surrounding ledge (i.e., five cubits above the altar’s base, six cubits from the ground. It was one cubit wide on all sides surrounding the altar.) for the burnt-offering of fowl was done above, when he came to the southwestern corner, where even it was close to the place of the ashes more than the other two corners, for the place of the ashes was near the ramp in the eastern side of the ramp and to the south of the altar.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:3:1)


**וּמַקִּיפִים דֶּרֶךְ שְׂמֹאל.** מַקִּיפִים אֶת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ בִּשְׁבִיל צָרְכֵי עֲבוֹדָה. כְּגוֹן לְמַתַּן דְּמֵי חַטָּאוֹת וּלְסִדּוּר מַעֲרָכָה אוֹ לְהַפֵּךְ אֵבָרִים בְּצִנּוֹרָא, וְיוֹרְדִים בְּמַעֲרָבוֹ שֶׁל כֶּבֶשׁ שֶׁהִיא שְׂמֹאל הָעוֹלִין לַמִּזְבֵּחַ בַּעֲלִיָּתָן:


ומקיפים דרך שמאל – cause to go around the Altar for the needs of the Divine Service. Such as for example to bringing of the blood of the sin-offerings and for the arrangement of the pile of wood on the altar or to turn over the limbs with a hook/curved pin. And the descend on the western side of the ramp which is on the left of those who ascend to the altar in their coming up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:3:2)


**חוּץ מִן הָעוֹלֶה לִשְׁלשָׁה דְבָרִים אֵלּוּ.** הַנַּעֲשִׂים בְּקֶרֶן מַעֲרָבִית דְּרוֹמִית, שֶׁהֵן נִסּוּךְ הַמַּיִם וְהַיַּיִן וְעוֹלַת הָעוֹף, שֶׁכְּשֶׁהוּא עוֹלֶה לַמִּזְבֵּחַ פּוֹנֶה לַסּוֹבֵב לִשְׂמֹאל בְּאוֹתָהּ הַקֶּרֶן, וּכְשֶׁגָּמַר חוֹזֵר עַל עָקֵב דֶּרֶךְ עֲלִיָּתוֹ. בְּנִסּוּךְ הַמַּיִם וְהַיַּיִן, שֶׁמָּא יִתְעַשְּׁנוּ בַּעֲשַׁן הַמִּזְבֵּחַ בְּעוֹד שֶׁהוּא פוֹנֶה דֶרֶךְ יָמִין וּמַקִּיף לִשְׂמֹאל וְיָפִיגוּ טַעֲמָן וְרֵיחָן, וַאֲנַן בָּעִינַן תְּמִימִים יִהְיוּ לָכֶם וְנִסְכֵּיהֶם, שֶׁיִהְיוּ הַנְּסָכִים תְּמִימִים. וְעוֹלַת הָעוֹף נַמִּי שֶׁמָּא תִתְעַשֵּׁן וְתָמוּת:


חוץ מן העולה לג' דברים אלו – that are done in the southwestern corner, which are libation of the water, and the wine and the burnt-offering of the fowl, and when he goes up to the altar, he turns to the surrounding ledge to the left in that corner and when he goes back the same way (see Tractate Zevahim 63b) on the path that he came up. With the libation of the water and wine, lest it be affected by smoke in the smoke of the altar while he is turning to the right and surrounding to the left and they will cause them to lose their taste and their fragrance, but we require (Numbers 28:31): “see that they are without blemish-with their libations,” that their libations will be pure. And the burnt-offering of the fowl also lest it be affected by the smoke and it dies.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:1)


**הָיָה מוֹלֵק אֶת רֹאשָׁהּ.** אוֹחֵז שְׁתֵּי גַפֶּיהָ בִּשְׁתֵּי אֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו זֶרֶת וּקְמִיצָה שֶׁל יָדוֹ הַשְּׂמָאלִית, וְהָעוֹף עַל גַּבֵּי יָדוֹ וּפָנָיו שֶׁל עוֹף לְצַד גַּב יָדוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הָעֹרֶף לְמַעְלָה, וּמוֹתֵחַ צַוָּארוֹ הַגָּרוֹן עַל רֹחַב שֶׁל שְׁתֵּי אֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו אֶצְבַּע וְאַמָּה. וּמוֹלֵק בְּצִפָּרְנוֹ. וְזוֹ אַחַת מֵעֲבוֹדוֹת קָשׁוֹת שֶׁבַּמִּקְדָּשׁ:


היה מולק את ראשה (pinch the bird’s neck with the finger nail) – he would hold its two wings with his two fingers the span from the little finger to the thumb of a spread hand and a fistful of the meal-offering in his left hand, and the bird on the side of his hand and the face of the bird to the side of the back of his hand, that the neck would be above, and he stretches/extends its neck of the throat on the width of his two fingers, the index finger and the arm and he pinches with his finger nail. And this is one of the hardest forms of Divine service that is in the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ מַבְדִּיל.** הָרֹאשׁ מִן הַגּוּף. אֶלָּא חוֹתֵךְ שִׁדְרָה וּמִפְרֶקֶת וְרֹב בָּשָׂר עִמּוֹ עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לַוֶּשֶׁט אוֹ לַקָּנֶה וְנוֹטֵל סִימָן אֶחָד לְבַדּוֹ וּמוֹלֵק, כְּדִכְתִיב וְלֹא יַבְדִּיל:


ואינו מבדיל – [and he doesn’t separate] the head from the body, but rather severs the spinal column and the nape and most of its flesh is with it until he reaches the gullet or the windpipe and takes one organ alone and pinches the bird’s neck with his finger nail, as it is written (Leviticus 5:8) “[He shall bring them to the priest, who shall offer first the one for the purification offering, pinching its head at the nape] without severing it.” (see Talmud Zevakhim 65b)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:3)


**וּמַזֶּה מִדָּמָהּ.** אוֹחֵז בָּעוֹף וּמַזֶּה. שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מַזֶּה לֹא בִּכְלִי וְלֹא בְּאֶצְבַּע אֶלָּא בְּגוּפוֹ שֶׁל עוֹף:


ומזה מדמה – he holds the bird/fowl and sprinkles. For he does not sprinkle neither with a utensil or with a finger, but with the body of the bird/fowl.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:4:4)


**הָיָה מִתְמַצֶּה.** שֶׁמְּקָרֵב בֵּית מְלִיקָתוֹ לַמִּזְבֵּחַ וְדֹחֲקוֹ בַּקִּיר וְהַדָּם מִתְמַצֶּה וְיוֹרֵד לַיְסוֹד. דִּבְחַטַּאת הָעוֹף כְּתִיב (ויקרא ה) וְהִזָּה מִדַּם הַחַטָּאת עַל קִיר הַמִּזְבֵּחַ וְהַנִּשְׁאָר בַּדָּם יִמָּצֵה אֶל יְסוֹד הַמִּזְבֵּחַ, אֵיזֶהוּ קִיר שֶׁהַשִּׁירַיִם שֶׁלּוֹ מִתְמַצִּין לַיְסוֹד, הֱוֵי אוֹמֵר זֶה קִיר הַתַּחְתּוֹן, דְּהַיְנוּ מִן הַחוּט וּלְמַטָּה. דְּאִי קִיר הָעֶלְיוֹן, דְּהַיְנוּ מִן הַחוּט וּלְמַעְלָה, פְּעָמִים שֶׁהָיָה מִתְמַצֶּה לַסּוֹבֵב, כֵּיוָן שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה לְמַעְלָה מִן הַסּוֹבֵב, שֶׁהַחוּט לְמַטָּה מִן הַסּוֹבֵב אַמָּה:


היה מתמצה (drains/empties out) – that he offers up the place in the bird’s neck to the altar and presses it on the wall and the blood drains out and goes down to the base [of the altar]. For regarding the sin-offering of the fowl/bird, it is written (Leviticus 5:9): “He shall sprinkle some of the blood of the purification offering on the side of the altar and what remains of the blood shall be drained out at the base of the altar,” Which wall do the remnants belong to and he drains out the blood to the base of the altar? I would say that this is the lowest wall, that is from the red line and below, for if it was the upper wall, that is from the red line and above, sometimes it (i.e., the blood) would drain out to the surrounding ledge, for since he would make it above the surrounding ledge that the red line is below the surrounding ledge by a cubit.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:1)


**עָלָה בַכֶּבֶשׁ.** לְפִי שֶׁעוֹלַת הָעוֹף נַעֲשֵׂית לְמַעְלָה, דְּלֹא כְתִיב בְּעוֹלַת הָעוֹף יְסוֹד, אֶלָּא בְּחַטַּאת הָעוֹף בִּלְבָד. וּבִבְהֵמָה אִפְּכָא, דְּחַטַּאת בְּהֵמָה נַעֲשֵׂית לְמַעְלָה דִּכְתִיב בָּהּ קַרְנוֹת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ, וְעוֹלַת בְּהֵמָה לְמַטָּה דִּכְתִיב (שם ד) אֶל יְסוֹד מִזְבַּח הָעוֹלָה, תָּלָה הַיְסוֹד בָּעוֹלָה:


עלה בכבש – since the burnt-offering of the fowl is done above, for it is not written [in Scripture] regarding the burnt-offering of the fowl/bird is in the base of the altar, but rather, only the sin-offering of the fowl/bird. But with the cattle it is the opposite, for the sin-offering of cattle is performed above [the red line] as it is written regarding it (Leviticus 4:18): “[Some of the blood he shall put] on the horns of the altar [which is before the LORD in the Tent of Meeting],” and the burnt offering of the cattle is below [the red line], as it is written (Leviticus 4:18): “ [and all the rest of the blood he shall pour out] at the base of the altar of burnt offering, [which is at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting],” [Scripture] assigned the base [of the altar] to the burnt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:2)


**בָּא לוֹ לְקֶרֶן דְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית.** לְפִי שֶׁהִיא קְרוֹבָה לְבֵית הַדֶּשֶׁן שֶׁשָּׁם מַשְׁלִיךְ הַמֻּרְאָה וְהַנּוֹצָה:


בא לו לקרן דרומית מזרחית – because it is close to the place of the ashes where they cast the crop of the birds and the down-covered skin [with the entrails that go along with it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:3)


**מִמּוּל עָרְפָּהּ.** מוּל הָרוֹאֶה אֶת עָרְפָּהּ. וְהוּא אֲחֹרֵי הַצַּוָּאר. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר בְּעוֹלָה מוּל עֹרֶף, לָמַדְנוּ בָהּ מוּל עֹרֶף מֵחַטָּאת:


ממול ערפה – opposite one who sees its neck. And it is the back of the neck. But even though it doesn’t say regarding a burnt-offering, “opposite the neck,” we learned from it, opposite the neck from the sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:4)


**וּמַבְדִּיל.** חוֹתֵךְ שְׁנֵי סִימָנִים. דְּמִדִּכְתִיב בְּחַטַּאת הָעוֹף וְלֹא יַבְדִּיל, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁבְּעוֹלַת הָעוֹף מַבְדִּיל:


ומבדיל – he severs two organs, from what is written in the sin-offering of the fowl/bird (Leviticus 1:17): “[The priest shall tear it open by its wings,] without severing it, [and turn it into smoke on the altar,” we learned that with the burnt-offering of a fowl/bird, he severs it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:5)


**וְהִקִּיף בֵּית מְלִיקָתוֹ לַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** מְקָרֵב וְדוֹחֵק בֵּית הַמְּלִיקָה בְּקִיר הַמִּזְבֵּחַ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְמַצֶּה הַדָּם:


והקיף בית מליקתו למזבח – he offers it up and presses the place in the bird’s neck to the wall of the altar in order that the blood will drain out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:6)


**וּסְפָגוֹ בְמֶלַח.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁאִיבָה וּלְקִיחָה, כְּמוֹ סוֹפֵג אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים. אַף כָּאן נוֹתֵן עָלָיו מֶלַח כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא הָרֹאשׁ מוֹשֵׁךְ וּמְקַבֵּל הַמֶּלַח. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, סְפָגוֹ בְּגִימֶ"ל כְּמוֹ סוֹפְקוֹ בְּקוּ"ף, לְשׁוֹן וַיִּסְפּוֹק אֶת כַּפָּיו (במדבר כד):


ספגו במלח – the language of drawn water and taking, like absorbing/receiving the forty [i.e., thirty-nine] lashes. Even here, he places upon it salt in order that the head will stretch and receive the salt. Another explanation: the word "ספגו" with a letter ג' is like סופק with a letter ק' (i.e., to supply an opportunity, to be sufficient) , the language of (Numbers 24:10): “[Engraged at Balaam,] Balak struck his hands together.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:7)


**מֻרְאָה.** זֶפֶק:


מוראה – the bird’s crop.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:8)


**נוֹצָה.** קוֹדֵר בּוֹ כְמִין אֲרֻבָּה וְנוֹטֵל כָּל הָעוֹר וְהַנּוֹצָה שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַזֶּפֶק עִם הַזֶּפֶק וּמַשְׁלִיךְ:


נוצה – he cuts through it like a sort of aperture and takes all the skin and the feathers/down (i.e., plumage) that is opposite the bird’s crop with the crop and casts it off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:5:9)


**שִׁסַּע.** אֶת הָעוֹף בֵּין אֲגַפִּים וְלֹא הָיָה מַבְדִּיל:


שסע – [tears open] the bird between the wings but he does not sever it.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:1)


**הִבְדִּיל בַּחַטָּאת.** שֶׁמָּלַק שְׁנֵי סִימָנִים בַּחַטָּאת:


הבדיל בחטאת – that he pinched the bird’s neck with his finger nail two organs with the sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:2)


**אוֹ לֹא הִבְדִּיל בָּעוֹלָה.** שֶׁמָּלַק בָּהּ סִימָן אֶחָד:


או לא הבדיל בעולה – that he pinched with it one organ.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:3)


**פָּסוּל.** דְּכָל שִׁנּוּי שֶׁהוּא לִפְנֵי עֲבוֹדַת הַדָּם פָּסוּל:


פסול – for every kind of difference prior to the [Divine] service with the blood is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:6:4)


**וְלֹא מִצָּה דַם הַגּוּף פְּסוּלָה.** דְּעִקַּר דָּמִים בַּגּוּף הוּא דִשְׁכִיחֵי:


ולא מיצה דם הגוף פסולה – for the essence of the blood is in the body, where it is found.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:1)


**חַטַּאת הָעוֹף שֶׁמְּלָקָהּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ.** מְלִיקָה בָּעוֹף בִּמְקוֹם שְׁחִיטָה בַּבְּהֵמָה. וּמִצּוּי בָּעוֹף בִּמְקוֹם זְרִיקַת הַדָּם בַּבְּהֵמָה. וְהַמַּחֲשָׁבָה הַפּוֹסֶלֶת בִּשְׁחִיטָה וּזְרִיקַת דָּם בַּבְּהֵמָה, פּוֹסֶלֶת בַּמְּלִיקָה וּבְמִצּוּי הַדָּם בָּעוֹף. אֲבָל קַבָּלָה וְהִלּוּךְ אֵין בָּעוֹף:


חטאת העוף שמלקה שלא לשמה – pinching of the neck of the fowl in place of the slaughtering for cattle, and the draining of the blood of the fowl in place of the sprinkling of the blood of the cattle. For the intention that invalidates the slaughtering and sprinkling of the blood in cattle invalidates the pinching of the neck of the bird and in the draining of the blood in a fowl/bird. But there is no receiving and bringing [of blood] in the fowl/bird.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:2)


**עוֹלַת הָעוֹף כְּשֵׁרָה.** כְּדִתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק קַמָּא גַּבֵּי בְהֵמָה, כָּל הַזְּבָחִים שֶׁנִּזְבְּחוּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָן כְּשֵׁרִים אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא עָלוּ לַבְּעָלִים לְשֵׁם חוֹבָה, חוּץ מִן הַפֶּסַח וּמִן הַחַטָּאת:


עולת העוף כשרה – as it is taught in the Mishnah at the beginning of the first chapter [of Tractate Zevakhim, Mishnah 1]: “All of the animal offerings which were slaughtered not for their own sake are valid [so that the blood is tossed and the entrails burned, etc.] , but they do not go to the owner’s credit in fulfillment of an obligation, except for the Passover offering and the sin-offering.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:3)


**לֶאֱכֹל דָּבָר שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לֶאֱכֹל.** חַטַּאת הָעוֹף:


לאכול דבר שדרכו לאכול – the sin-offering of the fowl/bird.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:4)


**לְהַקְטִיר דָּבָר שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לְהַקְטִיר.** עוֹלַת הָעוֹף:


להקטיר דבר שדרכו להקטיר – the burnt-offering of the fowl/bird.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 6:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/6:7:5)


**שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ.** אֵין מוֹצִיא עוֹלָה מִידֵי פִּגּוּל, לְפִי שֶׁהִיא כְשֵׁרָה שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ. וְכֻלַּהּ מַתְנִיתִין מְפֹרֶשֶׁת לְעֵיל פֶּרֶק ב':


שלא לשמה – we don’t remove a burnt-offering from improper intention, because it is fit/acceptable when it is not for its own sake. And all of our Mishnah is explained above in chapter two, [Mishnah four].



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:1)


**חַטַּאת הָעוֹף שֶׁעֲשָׂאָהּ לְמַטָּה.** מִחוּט הַסִּקְרָא, כְּהִלְכָתָהּ שֶׁהִיא נַעֲשֵׂית לְמַטָּה, כְּדַאֲמַרַן בְּפִרְקִין דִּלְעֵיל:


חטאת העוף שעשאה למטה – from the red line according to the Halakha that it is performed below, as we have stated in [Mishnah 4] in our chapter above (i.e., Chapter 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:2)


**כְּמַעֲשֵׂה חַטָּאת.** בְּהַזָּאָה וּמִצּוּי:


כמעשה חטאת – with sprinkling/tossing [of the blood] and draining [of the blood].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:3)


**לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשִּׁנָּה בְּסֵדֶר מְלִיקָה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁמָּלַק וְהִבְדִּיל, וּבְחַטָּאת נֶאֱמַר וְלֹא יַבְדִּיל:


ולשם חטאת – even though that he changed in the order/arrangement of the pinching of the head of the bird in such a manner that he pinched the bird’s neck with a finger nail and severed it, but with a sin-offering, it is stated (Leviticus 5:8): "לא יבדיל" / “[pinching its head at the nape] without severing it.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:4)


**כְּשֵׁרָה.** הוֹאִיל וְנַעֲשֵׂית לְמַטָּה וּבִשְׁאָר עֲבוֹדוֹת כְּהִלְכָתָהּ:


כשרה – because it was done below [the red line] and in the rest of the acts of Divine service it was performed in accordance with the Halakha (see Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 6, Mishnah 2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:5)


**כְּמַעֲשֵׂה חַטָּאת לְשֵׁם עוֹלָה וְכוּ'‏ פְּסוּלָה.** דְּחַטָּאת שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ פְּסוּלָה:


כמעשה חטאת לשם עולה וכו' פסולה – for a sin-offering not performed for its own sake is invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:1:6)


**כְּמַעֲשֵׂה כֻלָּם.** כְּלוֹמַר כְּמַעֲשֶׂה אֶחָד מִכֻּלָּם הָאֲמוּרִים כָּאן, וַאֲפִלּוּ כְּמַעֲשֵׂה חַטָּאת לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת, הוֹאִיל וַעֲשָׂאָהּ לְמַעְלָה פְּסוּלָה:


כמעשה כולם – that is to say, like the rites/actions of one of all of them that are mentioned here, and even according to the rite of the sin-offering for the sake of/for the name of the sin-offering, because it was performed above [the red line], it is invalid.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:1)


**עוֹלַת הָעוֹף שֶׁעֲשָׂאָהּ לְמַעְלָה.** כְּהִלְכָתָהּ שֶׁהִיא נַעֲשֵׂית לְמַעְלָה:


עולת העוף שעשאה למעלה – in accordance with the Halakha that it was performed above [the red line].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:2)


**כְּמַעֲשֵׂה עוֹלָה לְשֵׁם עוֹלָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשִּׁנָּה בִמְלִיקָתָהּ שֶׁלֹּא הִבְדִּיל, וּמִן הַדִּין הָיָה לוֹ לְהַבְדִּיל, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי כְּשֵׁרָה:


כמעשה עולה לשם עולה – even though he changed in the arrangement of the pinching of its head, that it would not be severed (see Leviticus 1:12: “The priest shall tear it open by its wings, לא יבדיל/without severing it, and turn it into smoke on the altar, upon the wood that is on the fire. It is a burnt offering, a gift, of pleasing odor to the LORD,” versus Leviticus 5:9 for the sin-offering: “He shall bring them to the priest, who shall offer first the one for the purification/sin-offering, pinching its head at the nape without severing it/ולא יבדיל), for according to the law, he should have severed it, even so, it is valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:3)


**כְּמַעֲשֵׂה עוֹלָה לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת כְּשֵׁרָה.** דְּכָל הַזְּבָחִים שֶׁנִּזְבְּחוּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָן כְּשֵׁרִים אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא עָלוּ לַבְּעָלִים לְשֵׁם חוֹבָה, חוּץ מִן הַפֶּסַח וּמִן הַחַטָּאת:


כמעשה עולה לשם חטאת כשרה – for all of the animal offerings that were offered up not for their own sake but rather that they did not go to the credit of its owns, except for the Passover offering and the sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:4)


**עֲשָׂאָהּ לְמַטָּה כְּמַעֲשֶׂה כֻלָּן.** כְּמַעֲשֶׂה אֶחָד מִכֻּלָּן, וַאֲפִלּוּ כְּמַעֲשֶׂה עוֹלָה לְשֵׁם עוֹלָה:


עשאה למטה כמעשה כולן – according to the rites of one of all of them, and even like the rite of the burnt-offering for the sake of/in the name of a burnt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:2:5)


**פְּסוּלָה.** הוֹאִיל וַעֲשָׂאָהּ לְמַטָּה פְּסוּלָה:


פסולה – [it is invalid] because he prepared it below [the red line].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:3:1)


**וְכֻלָּן אֵינָן מְטַמְּאוֹת בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ, מְלִיקָתָן מְטַהֲרְתָּן מִידֵי נְבֵלָה, וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה כְּדִין נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר שֶׁהָאוֹכְלָהּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע בָּהּ כְּגוֹן שֶׁתְּחָבָהּ לוֹ חֲבֵרוֹ בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה נִטְמָא טֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה לְטַמֵּא בְגָדִים שֶׁעָלָיו, אֲבָל אֵלּוּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין מְלִיקָתָן מַתִּירָתָן בַּאֲכִילָה מִפְּנֵי הַפְּסוּל שֶׁאֵרַע בָּהֶן, מִכָּל מָקוֹם מְטַהֲרָתָן וּמוֹצִיאָתָן מִידֵי נְבֵלָה:


וכולן אינן מטמאות בבית הבליעה (and all of them – which are invalid – do not impart uncleanness to the esophagus/gullet) – even though they were invalidated, their pinching of the bird’s head purifies them from having ritual impurity imparted by an un-slaughtered carcass, and they don’t become ritually impure in the gullet/esophagus according to the law of the un-slaughtered carcass of a pure bird for a person who consumes it, even though he did not touch it, as for example, that his fellow inserted it in his esophagus, it becomes ritually defiled in a more serious manner to defile the clothing that is upon him, but these, even though that their pinching of the bird’s head does not permit them for eating because of the defilement that occurred to them, nevertheless, it purifies them and removes them from being an un-slaughtered carcass.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:3:2)


**וּמוֹעֲלִין בָּהֶן.** הַנֶּהֱנֶה מֵהֶן בְּשָׁוֶה פְרוּטָה מֵבִיא קָרְבַּן מְעִילָה כְּדִין כָּל נֶהֱנֶה מִן הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ. וַאֲפִלּוּ חַטָּאת, הוֹאִיל וְנִפְסְלָה וְלֹא הָיָה בָהּ שְׁעַת הֶתֵּר לַכֹּהֲנִים, לֹא יָצְאָה מִידֵי מְעִילָה:


ומועלין בהן – he who derives a penny’s worth of benefit from them brings a sacrifice of sacrilege according to the law of all who derive benefit from that which is dedicated to the Temple. And even a sin-offering, because it was defiled and there was no time period of permissibility to the Kohanim, he did not depart from religious sacrilege.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:3:3)


**חוּץ מֵחַטַּאת הָעוֹף שֶׁעֲשָׂאָהּ לְמַטָּה כְּמַעֲשֵׂה חַטָּאת לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת.** דִּכְשֵׁרָה הִיא וְיֵשׁ בָּהּ שְׁעַת הֶתֵּר לַכֹּהֲנִים, וְשׁוּב אֵין בָּהּ מְעִילָה אֲפִלּוּ לְזָר:


חוץ מחטאת העוף שעשאה למטה כמעשה חטאת לשם חטאת – which is fit/valid and there is a period of permissibility for the Kohanim, and furthermore, there is no religious sacrilege involved even for a non-Kohen (literally: “a foreigner”).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר מוֹעֲלִים בָּהּ.** דְּהָא עוֹלָה הִיא וּמִי הוֹצִיאָהּ מִמְּעִילָתָהּ, וַהֲלֹא שְׁעַת הֶתֵּר לַכֹּהֲנִים לֹא הָיָה לָהּ:


ר' אליעזר אומר מועלים בה – for it is a burnt-offering, and who removed it from its religious sacrilege? For there wasn’t any permitted time for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר אֵין מוֹעֲלִים בָּהּ.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּשִׁנָּה שְׁמָהּ וּמַעֲשֶׂיהָ וּמְקוֹמָהּ לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת, נַעֲשֵׂית חַטָּאת:


רבי יהושע אומר אין מועלים בה – for since he changed its name and its action and its place for the sake/name of the sin-offering, it became a sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:3)


**וּמַה חַטַּאת הָעוֹף שֶׁאֵין מוֹעֲלִים בָּהּ.** כְּשֶׁשָּׁחֲטָהּ לִשְׁמָהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי נֶאֱכֶלֶת לַכֹּהֲנִים:


ומה חטאת העוף שאין מועלים בה – when he slaughtered it for its sake/name, for it is consumed by the Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:4)


**שִׁנָּה אֶת שְׁמָהּ.** נִפְסְלָה וְלֹא בָאָה לִכְלַל הֶתֵּר:


שינה את שמה – it was disqualified and it does not come to be included in that which is permissible.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:5)


**מוֹעֲלִים בָּהּ.** דְּהָא רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ גּוּפֵיהּ לֹא פָּלֵיג עָלֶיהָ:


מועלים בה – for Rabbi Yehoshua himself did not dispute this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:6)


**לְדָבָר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ מְעִילָה.** לְשֵׁם עוֹלָה:


לדבר שיש בו מעילה – for the sake/name of a burnt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:7)


**שֶׁשָּׁחֲטָן בַּדָּרוֹם.** לְשֵׁם שְׁלָמִים, יוֹכִיחוּ:


ששחטן בדרום – for the name/sake of a peace-offering will prove it (see also Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 1 - as this sacrifice is supposed to be offered in the north).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:8)


**שֶׁשִּׁנָּה שְׁמָן וּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶן לְדָבָר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מְעִילָה.** שֶׁקָּדָשִׁים קַלִּים אֵין מְעִילָה אֶלָּא בְּאֵמוּרֵיהֶן:


ששינה שמן ומעשיהן לדבר שאין בו מעילה – for the Lesser Holy Things do not have religious sacrilege other than with their portions of the offerings that are consumed on the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:9)


**וּמוֹעֲלִים בָּהֶן.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ בְּשִׁנּוּי מְקוֹמָן וְלֹא הוֹצִיאָתָן זְרִיקָתָן מִידֵי מְעִילָה:


ומועלים בהן – because they were disqualified with the change of their place and their sprinkling/tossing of the blood did not remove them from religious sacrilege.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:10)


**אִסּוּר וְהֶתֵּר.** קָדָשִׁים קַלִּים יֵשׁ בְּאֵמוּרֵיהֶן מִשּׁוּם מְעִילָה וְאֵין בִּבְשָׂרָן מִשּׁוּם מְעִילָה:


איסור והיתר – the Lesser Holy Things there is religious sacrilege with the portions of their offerings that are consumed on the altar, but not with their flesh because of religious sacrilege.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:4:11)


**בְּדָבָר שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ הֶתֵּר.** בְּחַטַּאת הָעוֹף שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ צַד מְעִילָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


בדבר שכולו היתר – with the sin-offering of birds which has no side of religious sacrilege. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:1)


**מָלַק בִּשְׂמֹאל.** דִּמְלִיקָתוֹ פְסוּלָה. שֶׁכָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר אֶצְבַּע וּכְהֻנָּה אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא שֶׁל יָמִין:


מלק בשמאל – that his pinching of the bird’s head is invalid. For every place where it states, “finger”/אצבע and “priesthood”/כהונה it is none other than the right [hand].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:2)


**אוֹ בַלַּיְלָה.** דְּאֵין מְלִיקָה בַלַּיְלָה, דְּרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר בְּיוֹם צַוֹּתוֹ:


או בלילה – for there is pinching of the bird’s head at night, for the All-Merciful stated (Leviticus 7:38): “[with which the LORD charged Moses on Mount Sinai,] when He commanded [by day] (not included in the new Jewish Publication Society translation but found explicitly in the Hebrew ) /ביום צותו [that the Israelites present their offerings to the LORD, in the wilderness of Sinai].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:3)


**אֵינָן מְטַמְּאִין.** הוֹאִיל וּפְסוּלָן בַּקֹּדֶשׁ וְאַהֲנֵי מְלִיקָה דִּידְהוּ שֶׁאִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ:


אינן מטמאין – for since they are disqualified in Holy Things and even their pinching of the bird’s head that if they went up, but did not descend (see also, Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 9, Mishnah 2, that the pinching of the bird’s head purifies them).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:4)


**שָׁחַט חֻלִּין בִּפְנִים וְקָדָשִׁים בַּחוּץ אֵין מְטַמְּאִין בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲסוּרִים בַּאֲכִילָה:


שחט חולין בפנים וקדשים בחוץ אין מטמאין בבית הבליעה – and even though they are forbidden to be consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:5)


**מָלַק בְּסַכִּין.** לָאו מְלִיקָה הִיא וְלָאו שְׁחִיטָה הִיא, דְּהָא מְלִיקָה בְּצִפֹּרֶן הִיא בְּעַצְמוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן:


מלק בסכין – it is not the [proper] pinching of the bird’s head (i.e., neck), and it is not [proper] ritual slaughter, for the pinching of the bird’s neck is with the fingernail by the Kohen himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:6)


**מָלַק חֻלִּין בִּפְנִים.** דְּלֹא שַׁיָּךְ מְלִיקָה בְּחֻלִּין אֶלָּא בְּקָדָשִׁים בִּפְנִים, וְהַמּוֹלֵק חֻלִּין בְּכָל מָקוֹם אוֹ קָדָשִׁים בַּחוּץ הָוֵי כְּאִלּוּ נוֹחֵר אוֹ מְעַקֵּר, וְאֵין אוֹתָהּ מְלִיקָה מוֹצִיאָה מִידֵי נְבֵלָה:


מלק חולין בפנים – for the pinching of the bird’s head (i.e., neck) does not belong with unconsecrated [birds] but rather with [birds] that are sanctified inside [at the altar], and the person who pinches the bird’s neck with the fingernail for a profane purpose in any place, or Holy Things outside [the altar], it is as if he perforates (i.e., kills by stabbing) or mutilates it, and this pinching of the bird’s head does not remove it from being a carrion (i.e., an animal that has died a natural death, not slaughtered according to the ritual rules).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:7)


**תּוֹרִין שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ זְמַנָּן.** תּוֹרִים גְּדוֹלִים כְּשֵׁרִים, קְטַנִּים פְּסוּלִים. בְּנֵי יוֹנָה, קְטַנִּים כְּשֵׁרִים, גְּדוֹלִים פְּסוּלִים:


תורין שלא הגיעו זמנן – large turtle doves, which are kosher/fit, small ones are invalid. Pigeons – small ones are kosher/fit, large ones are invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:8)


**וְשֶׁיָּבַשׁ גַּפָּה.** דִּמְחֻסָּר אֵבָר פָּסוּל אַף בָּעוֹפוֹת. דְּהָא דְּקַיְמָא לָן אֵין תַּמּוּת וְזִכְרוּת בְּעוֹפוֹת, הָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּגוֹן דּוֹקִין שֶׁבָּעַיִן שֶׁאֵין מוּמָן נִכָּר, אֲבָל מְחֻסַּר אֵבָר, הַקְרִיבֵהוּ נָא לְפֶחָתֶךָ:


ושיבש גפה – that it is missing a limb, it is invalid, even with birds. For we hold that there is no unblemished condition or male sex with regard to birds, these words, as for example regarding a withered spot in the eye/cataract that their blemishes are not recognized, but that which is missing a limb, (Malachi 1:8): “Just offer it to your governor.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:9)


**מְטַמֵּא בְבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** דְּלֹא מַהֲנֵי מְלִיקָה דִּידְהוּ לְמִידִי, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ עָלוּ יֵרְדוּ:


מטמא בבית הבליעה – that their pinching of the head has no effect at all, for even if they went up, they descended.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:10)


**כֹּל שֶׁאֵין פְּסוּלָן בַּקֹּדֶשׁ.** שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ קֹדֶם שֶׁבָּאוּ לָעֲזָרָה. וְכָל שֶׁאֵין פְּסוּלָן בַּקֹּדֶשׁ אָמְרִינַן לְקַמָּן בְּפֶרֶק הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְקַדֵּשׁ, דְּאִם עָלָה יֵרֵד. אֲבָל אוֹתָן שֶׁפְּסוּלָן בַּקֹּדֶשׁ כְּגוֹן הַקֳּדָשִׁים הָרְאוּיִין אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ בַּעֲבוֹדוֹתֵיהֶן, אֵין מְטַמְּאִין, דְּהָא אַהֲנֵי לְהוּ מְלִיקָה דְּאִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ:


כל שאין פסולן בקודש – that it became invalid before they came to the Temple courtyard. And everything where its becoming invalid did not happen in the Holy area, we state further on in the Chapter [Nine], “The altar sanctifies that which is appropriate to it”/"המזבח מקדש" (see Mishnah 5), “And what are those things which, if they have gone up, should go down.” But those things that were invalidated in the Holy Areas, as for example, the Holy Things that were appropriate but they were invalidated in their Divine Service, they don’t defile, for the pinching of the bird’s head was beneficial to them for if they went up, they would not come down (see Mishnah 2 of that chapter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:11)


**כָּל הַפְּסוּלִים.** כְּגוֹן זָר וְאוֹנֵן וּטְבוּל יוֹם וּמְחֻסַּר כִּפּוּרִים וְכָל אוֹתָן הַשְּׁנוּיִין בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ב':


כל הפסולים – as for example, a “foreigner,” (i.e., non-Kohen), and someone prior to the burial of a kinsman, a person who immersed himself on that day and someone lacking atonement (i.e., a woman after childbirth, a person suffering a flux, a healed leper, etc.) – all of those that are taught at the beginning of chapter 2 [of Tractate Zevakhim].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:5:12)


**וְאֵינָן מְטַמְּאוֹת בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין מְלִיקָתָן מַתֶּרֶת קָדָשִׁים בַּאֲכִילָה, מִכָּל מָקוֹם מוֹצִיאָתָן מִידֵי נְבֵלָה:


ואינן מטמאות בבית הבליעה – for even though their pinching of the bird’s neck does not permit Holy Things to be eaten, nevertheless, it removes them from being considered an un-slaughtered animal carcass.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:1)


**אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה בְבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** דִּמְלִיקָתָהּ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ מִידֵי נְבֵלָה:


אינה מטמאה בבית הבליעה – that the pinching of the bird’s head purifies it from being considered an un-slaughtered animal carcass.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר מְטַמְּאָה בְבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְלָא אַהֲנֵי שְׁחִיטָה בְּעוֹף שֶׁל חֻלִּין וְלֹא מְלִיקָה בְּקָדָשִׁים בִּטְרֵפָה בָּעוֹף לְהוֹצִיאָהּ מִידֵי נְבֵלָה:


ר' יהודה אומר מטמאה בבית הבליעה – for Rabbi Yehuda holds that the ritual slaughtering of an unconsecrated bird does not have any effect nor the pinching of the bird’s neck which is a Holy Thing that is deemed torn in a bird to remove it from being considered an un-slaughtered animal carcass.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:3)


**אֵינוֹ דִין שֶׁתְּהֵא שְׁחִיטָה כוּ'.** וְכֵיוָן דְּיָלַפְתְּ שֶׁשְּׁחִיטָה מְטַהֵר אֶת הַטְּרֵפָה בָּעוֹף בְּחֻלִּין מִקַּל וָחֹמֶר יָלְפִינַן מְלִיקַת קָדָשִׁים מִנַּהּ בְּבִנְיַן אָב, מַה מָּצִינוּ בִּשְׁחִיטָה כוּ'‏ אַף מְלִיקָה הַמַּכְשֶׁרֶת בַּאֲכִילָה תְּטַהֵר טְרֵיפָתָהּ מִטֻּמְאָתָהּ:


אינו דין שתהא שחיטה כו' – and since you derived that ritual slaughter purifies the bird with a condition that will cause it to die within twelve months (i.e., an animal torn apart by a beast of prey or afflicted with a severe organic disease or congenital defect) with regard to an unconsecrated bird from an a fortiori inference, we derive the pinching of a bird’s head of Holy Things from it through an analogy based upon an induction (i.e., what do we find with regard to – with regard to similar cases…? Analogy based upon one verse or analogy based upon two verses), just as we found through proper slaughter, etc., even the pinching of a bird’s neck that makes it appropriate for eating, it will purify its being viewed as having a condition that will cause it to die within twelve months from its defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/7:6:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר דַּיָּהּ כוּ'.** כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא אַשְׁכְּחַן בְּהֶדְיָא דִּשְׁחִיטָה מוֹצִיאָה מִידֵי נְבֵלָה בָּעוֹף, אֶלָּא מִקַּל וָחֹמֶר מִנִּבְלַת בְּהֵמָה אַתָּה בָא לִלְמֹד, דַּיָּהּ שֶׁתְּהֵא כְּנִבְלַת בְּהֵמָה שֶׁשְּׁחִיטָתָהּ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ מִידֵי נְבֵלָה וְלֹא מְלִיקָתָהּ, דְּדַיּוֹ לַבָּא מִן הַדִּין לִהְיוֹת כַּנִּדּוֹן. וְשָׁלֹשׁ מַחֲלוֹקוֹת בַּדָּבָר, רַבִּי מֵאִיר סָבַר, אֶחָד שְׁחִיטָה וְאֶחָד מְלִיקָה מוֹצִיאָה בָּעוֹף מִידֵי נְבֵלָה. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר, בֵּין שְׁחִיטָה בֵּין מְלִיקָה לֹא מַהֲנֵי בִּטְרֵפָה בָּעוֹף לְהוֹצִיאָהּ מִידֵי נְבֵלָה. וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי סָבַר, שְׁחִיטָה מְהַנְּיָא, מְלִיקָה לֹא מְהַנְּיָא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר דיה כו' – since we don’t find explicitly that ritual slaughter removes it from being a bird with a condition that will cause it to die within twelve months, other than through an a fortiori inference from the condition that will cause an animal to die within twelve months (i.e., an animal torn apart by a beast of prey or afflicted with a severe organic disease or congenital defect), it comes to learn, it is sufficient that it will be like an animal with a condition that will cause it to die within twelve months whose ritual slaughter will purify it from being considered as torn apart or afflicted with a congenital defect or a severe organic disease and not through the pinching of its neck, that it is sufficient that the law which is derived by conclusion ad majus to be as strict as the law from which it is derived (i.e., you cannot go beyond the latter). And there are three arguments in the manner: Rabbi Meir holds that both ritual slaughter and pinching the neck of the bird remove it from being considered having a condition that will cause the animal to die within twelve months. But Rabbi Yehuda holds that whether one performs ritual slaughter or the pinching of the neck of the bird, it will not affect the fact of the animal having a condition that will cause it to die within twelve months to remove it from being considered an un-slaughtered carcass. But Rabbi Yossi holds that ritual slaughtering is effective; pinching of the bird’s neck is not effective. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:1)


**כָּל הַזְּבָחִים שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ בְחַטָּאוֹת הַמֵּתוֹת.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, כָּל הַזְּבָחִים שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ בָהֶם חַטָּאוֹת הַמֵּתוֹת, אוֹ שׁוֹר הַנִּסְקָל:


כל שזבחים שנתערבו בחטאות המתות – this is what he said: all the animal offerings that were mixed up with sin-offerings left to die or with the ox to be stoned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ אַחַת בְּרִבּוֹא יָמוּתוּ כֻלָּן.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִתְעָרְבוּ בְּאִסּוּרֵי הֲנָאָה אֵין תַּקָּנָה לִפְדּוֹתָם דְּנֵימָא יִרְעוּ עַד שֶׁיִּסְתָּאֲבוּ. וְלֹא בָטְלֵי בְּרֻבָּא, דְּבַעֲלֵי חַיִּים חֲשִׁיבֵי וְלֹא בָטְלֵי. וְחַטָּאוֹת הַמֵּתוֹת הֵן חֲמִשָּׁה, וְאֵלּוּ הֵן, וְלַד חַטָּאת, וּתְמוּרַת חַטָּאת, וְחַטָּאת שֶׁמֵּתוּ בְעָלֶיהָ, וְשֶׁכִּפְּרוּ בְעָלֶיהָ בְּאַחֶרֶת, וְשֶׁעָבְרָה שְׁנָתָהּ. אֵלּוּ חָמֵשׁ חַטָּאוֹת מַנִּיחִין אוֹתָן בְּמָקוֹם סָגוּר עַד שֶׁיָּמוּתוּ מֵאֲלֵיהֶן, וְהֵן נִקְרָאִין חַטָּאוֹת הַמֵּתוֹת:


אפילו אחת בריבוי ימותו כולן – for since they had become mixed up with that from which are forbidden to derive benefit, there is no remedy to redeem them that we say that they should graze until they become unfit. And they are not abrogated through a majority, for they are considered living creatures and are not nullified. And sin-offerings left to die are five in number, and they are these: the offspring of a sin-offering, an animal substituted for a sin-offering, a sin-offering whose owners have died, a sin-offering whose owners have already gained atonement through another offering and a sin-offering of sheep or goats that is more than a year old. These five sin-offerings we place them in a closed place until they die on their own, and they are called “sin-offerings left to die.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:3)


**בְּשׁוֹר שֶׁנֶּעֶבְדָה בוֹ עֲבֵרָה.** שֶׁהוּא נִפְסַל בָּהּ לְהַקְרָבָה. כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֵמִית אֶת הָאָדָם עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד שֶׁאֵינוֹ אָסוּר לְהֶדְיוֹט, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִסְקָל אֶלָּא עַל פִּי שְׁנֵי עֵדִים:


בשור שנעבדה בו עבירה – that it (i.e., the ox) was disqualified for offering as a sacrifice. As for example, that it put a person to death on the testimony of a single witness who is not prohibited to a commoner, because it is not stoned other than through two witnesses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:4)


**אוֹ עַל פִּי הַבְּעָלִים.** שֶׁבָּאוּ בְעָלִים לְבֵית דִּין וְהוֹדוּ שֶׁהֵמִית, דְּאִפְטַר לֵיהּ מִסְּקִילָה, דְּמוֹדֶה בִּקְנָס פָּטוּר, אֲבָל לְגָבוֹהַּ נֶאֱסָר, דְּקַיְמָא לָן, וּמִן הַצֹּאן, לְהוֹצִיא אֶת הַנּוֹגֵחַ:


או על פי הבעלים – that its owners came to the Jewish court and admitted that it had put a person to death, that it would be exempted from stoning that they admit to a fine, it would be exempt. But to On-High, it would be prohibited, for we hold (Leviticus 1:2): “[When any of you presents an offering of cattle to the LORD, he shall choose his offering from the herd] or from the flock/ומן הצאן,” to exclude that [ox] which gores.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:5)


**וְרוֹבֵעַ וְנִרְבָּע.** עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד, אוֹ עַל פִּי הַבְּעָלִים, אוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּגְמַר דִּינוֹ, אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה אוֹתוֹ שֶׁהֵמִית וְהָרוֹבֵעַ וְהַנִּרְבָּע [שֶׁל] נָכְרִי. שֶׁנֶּאֱסָר לְגָבוֹהַּ וְלֹא לְהֶדְיוֹט:


ברובע ונרבע – by the testimony of one witness, or by the owners, or prior to the completion of the court case, or that [animal] which killed someone or the ox which had a sexual relation with a human being (i.e., a non-Jew) or an ox with which a non-Jewish person had sexual relations, which is forbidden to the All-High, but not to the commoner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:6)


**מֻקְצֶה.** שֶׁהִפְרִישׁוּהוּ לְקָרְבָּן לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, וְנֶעֱבָד נַמִּי, שְׁנֵיהֶם מֻתָּרִים לְהֶדְיוֹט:


מוקצה – that he had separated for a sacrifice to idolatry and was also worshipped, both of them are permitted to a commoner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:7)


**וּבְאֶתְנָן וּבִמְחִיר.** כָּל הָנֵי חֻלִּין נִינְהוּ, וְזֶבַח אֶחָד מְעֹרָב בָּהֶן, כֻּלָּן יִרְעוּ. שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לִשְׁחֹט אֶחָד מֵהֶם בְּלֹא מוּם, שֶׁמָּא זֶהוּ הַזֶּבַח, וְהַשּׁוֹחֵט קָדָשִׁים בַּחוּץ חַיָּב כָּרֵת. וּלְמָכְרָן לְצָרְכֵי עוֹלוֹת אִי אֶפְשָׁר, שֶׁהֲרֵי פְּסוּלִים:


באתנן ומחיר – all of these are unconsecrated animals, and one animal sacrifice is mixed with them, all of them should sent out to pasture. For it is impossible to ritually slaughter one of them without a blemish, lest that this one is the animal offering. For the person who ritually slaughters Holy Things outside [the Temple courtyard] is liable for extirpation. But to sell them for the needs of burnt offerings is impossible, for they are invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:8)


**וְיָבִיא בִדְמֵי הַיָּפֶה שֶׁבָּהֶן מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין.** אִם חַטָּאת חַטָּאת וְאִם עוֹלָה עוֹלָה. וְיִקַּח מָעוֹת כְּשִׁעוּר דְּמֵי הַיָּפֶה שֶׁבְּכֻלָּן וְיֹאמַר, כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁהוּא הַזֶּבַח יְהֵא מְחֻלָּל עַל הַמָּעוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


ויביא בדמי היפה שבהן מאותו המין – if it was a sin-offering, [let him purchase] another sin-offering; if it was burnt-offering, [let him purchase] another burnt-offering. And he should the monies according to the measurement of the worth of the best of them and state: “Every place which is animal offering will be redeemed by these monies.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:9)


**נִתְעָרֵב.** זֶבַח אֶחָד בְּחֻלִּין כְּשֵׁרִים הַרְבֵּה:


נתערב – one animal offering in many unconsecrated fit/kosher animals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:10)


**יִמָּכְרוּ הַחֻלִּין.** כֻּלָּן:


ימכרו החולין – all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:1:11)


**לְצָרְכֵי אוֹתוֹ הַמִּין שֶׁהָיָה הַזֶּבַח שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בָּהֶן.** וְנִמְצְאוּ כֻּלָּן קָדָשִׁים מִמִּין אֶחָד אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵין יָדוּעַ מִי בְּעָלָיו שֶׁל כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַזֶּבַח הָרִאשׁוֹן שֵׁם בְּעָלָיו עָלָיו וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אֵיזֶהוּ. וְתַקָּנָתוֹ, שֶׁיַּקְרִיב כָּל אֶחָד לְשֵׁם מִי שֶׁהוּא וְיֹאמַר כְּשֶׁיַּקְרִיב כָּל אֶחָד, הֲרֵי הוּא לְשֵׁם בְּעָלִים שֶׁלּוֹ:


לצריכי אותו המין – that the one animal offering was mixed in with them, and it was found that all of them are Holy Things from one species but it is not known who are the owners of each and every one, for the first animal offering – the name of its owners was upon it, but it is not known which it is. And the remedy, that each of one of them should be offered as a sacrifice in the name of someone and he should state that he will offer each of them as a sacrifice, behold this one is in the name of its owners.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:1)


**מִין בְּמִינוֹ.** חַטָּאת בְּחַטָּאת, עוֹלָה בְּעוֹלָה, שְׁלָמִים בִּשְׁלָמִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁהֵן שֶׁל בְּעָלִים הַרְבֵּה:


מין במינו – a sin-offering with a sin-offering, a burnt-offering with a burnt-offering, a peace-offering with a peace-offering, but they belong to many owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:2)


**יַקְרִיב כָּל אֶחָד לְשֵׁם מִי שֶּׁהוּא.** וְלֹא יַזְכִּיר שֵׁם אָדָם. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי בְּקָרְבָּנוֹת שֶׁל נָשִׁים דְּלֹא בָעֵי סְמִיכָה, דִּכְתִיב בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְסָמַךְ, בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל סוֹמְכִים וְאֵין בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל סוֹמְכוֹת. אֲבָל בְּקָרְבָּן אֲנָשִׁים דְּבָעֵי סְמִיכָה, לֹא:


זה יקריב כל אחד לשם מי שהוא – and he doesn’t mention the name of person. These words concern the sacrifice of women that don’t require the placing of hands on the head of an offering, as it is written )Leviticus 1:2,4) “Speak to the Israelite people, [and say unto them]…He shall lay his hand [upon the head of the burnt offering],” Israelite men place their hands on the head of an offering, but women of the Israelite people do not place their hands on the head of an offering (see also Talmud Eruvin 96b, Talmud Hagigah 16b and Kiddushin 36a), but not on a sacrifice of men that require placing of their hands on their head of the offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:3)


**מִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ יִרְעוּ.** שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר בְּהַקְרָבָה, שֶׁחֲלוּקִין בְּמַתַּן דָּמִים אוֹ בְהַקְטָרָתָן אוֹ בַאֲכִילָתָן:


מין כשאינו מניו ירעו – for it is impossible to offer it up as a sacrifice, for they are divided in the giving of monies or their burning [of the fats and limbs] or their consumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:4)


**וְיַפְסִיד הַמּוֹתָר מִבֵּיתוֹ.** שֶׁאִם מָכַר אֶחָד מֵהֶן בִּשְׁנַיִם וְהָאַחֵר בְּאֶחָד, צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא מִכָּל מִין בִּשְׁנַיִם, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין יָדוּעַ מֵאֵיזֶה מִין הוּא אוֹתוֹ שֶׁנִּמְכָּר בִּשְׁנַיִם, וְנִמְצָא מַפְסִיד אֶחָד מִבֵּיתוֹ:


ויפסיד המותר מביתו (let him lose [make up] the added difference from his own property) – for if one sold for two and the other sold for one, he has to bring from every species for two, since it is not known from which species it is that which was sold for two, and it is found that he loses one from his own property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:5)


**נִתְעָרְבוּ בִבְכוֹר וּבְמַעֲשֵׂר.** בְּהֵמָה. שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם פִּדְיוֹן שֶׁתָּחוּל קְדֻשָּׁתוֹ עַל דְּמֵיהֶן וְיֵצְאוּ הֵן לְחֻלִּין, אֶלָּא נֶאֱכָלִין בְּמוּמָן, הַבְּכוֹר נֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַמַּעֲשֵׂר לַבְּעָלִים:


נתערבו בבכור ובמעשר – [if they were mixed up with a firstling or the tithe] of cattle. For they don’t have redemption that its holiness will be redeemed on their monetary value and they (i.e., the animals) will leave to become unconsecrated, but rather, they are consumed with their blemishes, the firstling is consumed by the Kohanim and the tithe by its owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:6)


**כֻּלָּן יִרְעוּ עַד שֶׁיִּסְתָּאֲבוּ.** שֶׁיִּפֹּל בָּהֶן מוּם. וְיֹאמַר, כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁהוּא הַזֶּבַח יִתְחַלֵּל עַל הַמָּעוֹת הַלָּלוּ. וּמֻתָּרִים לְאָכְלָן, וּבִלְבָד שֶׁיֵּאָכְלוּ בְּתוֹרַת בְּכוֹר וּמַעֲשֵׂר, שֶׁלֹּא יִמְכְּרֵם בָּאִטְלִיז וְלֹא יִשְׁקְלֵם בַּלִּיטְרָא. וּמִדְּמֵי הַזֶּבַח יַקְרִיב מִינוֹ שֶׁל זֶבַח:


כולן ירעו עד שיסתאבו – when a blemish befalls them. And he will say, every place where the animal offering becomes redeemed for these monies. And they are permitted to eat them, as long as that they eat them according to the law of the firstling and the tithe, that we will not sell them in shops/a mercantile fair and they will not weigh them with a measure of capacity/Litra. And from the monies of the animal offering, he will offer up his species of the offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:7)


**הַכֹּל יְכוֹלִים לְהִתְעָרֵב.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם נִתְעָרְבוּ יֵשׁ סָפֵק בְּתַעֲרָבְתָּן:


הכל יכולים להתערב – that is to say if they were combined, there is a doubt in their mixture.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:2:8)


**חוּץ מִן הַחַטָּאת עִם הָאָשָׁם.** שֶׁהַחַטָּאת אֵינָהּ לְעוֹלָם אַיִל, וְאָשָׁם אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא אַיִל. וְאִי אֲשַׁם מְצֹרָע וַאֲשַׁם נָזִיר הוּא שֶׁהֵן בָּאִים כְּבָשִׂים, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הֵן זְכָרִים, וְהַחַטָּאת אֵינָהּ בָּאָה אֶלָּא נְקֵבָה. אֲבָל חַטָּאת וְעוֹלָה מִתְעָרְבִים, כְּגוֹן שְׂעִיר נָשִׂיא בִּשְׂעִיר עוֹלַת נְדָבָה. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן חַטָּאת וּשְׁלָמִים, שֶׁהַכֹּל קָרֵב שְׁלָמִים בֵּין זְכָרִים בֵּין נְקֵבוֹת. וְכֵן אַיִל שֶׁל עוֹלַת נְדָבָה בְּאָשָׁם:


חוץ מן החטאת עם האשם – for the sin-offering is never a ram, and the guilt-offering is not anything but a ram. But if it they are the guilt-offerings of a leper or of a Nazirite, they come from lambs, nevertheless, they are male, but the sin-offering does not come other than from a female. But the sin-offering and the burnt-offering combine, such as the goat of the chieftain with the goat of the free-will burnt-offering. And all the more so, the sin-offering and the peace-offering, for all are offered as peace-offerings, whether male or whether female. And similarly, the ram of the free-will burnt-offering with a guilt-offering.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:1)


**אָשָׁם שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בִּשְׁלָמִים.** שֶׁמַתַּן דָּמוֹ שֶׁל אָשָׁם וּשְׁלָמִים שָׁוֶה:


אשם שנתערב בשלמים (see previous Mishnah where this subject is discussed) – for the giving of the blood of the guilt-offering and the peace-offering is similar (i.e., in the north – see Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 3).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:2)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם יִשָּׁחֲטוּ בַצָּפוֹן.** דִּשְׁלָמִים שְׁחִיטָתָן בְּכָל מָקוֹם וִיכוֹלִים נַמִּי לְשָׁחֲטָן בַּצָּפוֹן בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁשּׁוֹחֲטִים הָאָשָׁם:


שניהם ישחטו בצפון – but peace-offerings are ritually slaughtered in every place and one is able also to slaughter them in the om the north in the place when he slaughters the guilt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:3)


**כֶּחָמוּר שֶׁבָּהֶן.** לִפְנִים מִן הַקְּלָעִים לְזִכְרֵי כְהֻנָּה לְיוֹם וְלַיְלָה. וְלֹא כַּשְּׁלָמִים הַקַּלִּים שֶׁנֶּאֱכָלִים בְּכָל הָעִיר לְכָל אָדָם לִשְׁנֵי יָמִים וְלַיְלָה אֶחָד. וּבַעַל הַשְּׁלָמִים יָצָא יְדֵי נִדְרוֹ, דְּהָא שְׁלָמִים לִשְׁמָן הֵן קְרֵבִין, שֶׁמַּקְרִיב כָּל אֶחָד לְשֵׁם מִי שֶׁהוּא:


כחמור שבהן – inside from the enclosures in the Temple courtyard for male Kohanim during eh day and at night. But not like the Lesser Peace-Offerings that are consumed throughout the city for all people for two days and one night (see Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 8). And the owner of the peace-offerings fulfilled his vow, for peace-offerings are sacrificed for their own sake, each person offers it for the sake of someone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:4)


**אֵין מְבִיאִין קָדָשִׁים לְבֵית הַפְּסוּל.** שֶׁמְּמַעֵט אֶת אֲכִילַת שְׁלָמִים בֵּין בְּאוֹכְלֵיהֶן בֵּין בִּזְמַן אֲכִילָתָן וּמְבִיאָן לִידֵי נוֹתָר. אֶלָּא יִרְעוּ וְיִסְתָּאֲבוּ וְיִמָּכְרוּ כִּדְאָמְרִינַן גַּבֵּי קָדָשִׁים בְּקָדָשִׁים מִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ, וְיָבִיא בִדְמֵי הַיָּפֶה שֶׁבָּהֶן כוּ'. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אין מביאין קדשים לבית הפסול – that he diminishes the consumption of peace-offerings whether concerning those who eats them or whether the time of their consumption and brings them to residue/remnant. But rather, they should let them graze until they become unfit/blemished and then they should be sold as we stated regarding Holy Things with Holy Things where it is one type mixed with another type, and he should bring with the proceeds of the best of them [of that kind]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:3:5)


**חֲתִיכוֹת בַּחֲתִיכוֹת.** אִם נִתְעָרְבוּ חֲתִיכוֹת קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים בַּחֲתִיכוֹת שֶׁל קָדָשִׁים קַלִּים, אוֹ חֲתִיכוֹת שֶׁל קָדָשִׁים קַלִּים הַנֶּאֱכָלִים לְיוֹם אֶחָד כְּגוֹן שַׁלְמֵי תוֹדָה עִם חֲתִיכוֹת שֶׁל קָדָשִׁים קַלִּים הַנֶּאֱכָלִים לִשְׁנֵי יָמִים וְלַיְלָה אֶחָד דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁאָר שְׁלָמִים, בְּהָא מוֹדוּ רַבָּנָן לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּיֵאָכְלוּ כֶּחָמוּר שֶׁבָּהֶן, דְּמַאי אִית לֵיהּ לְמֶעְבַּד:


חתיכות בחתיכות – if pieces [of meat] of the Holy of Holies were mixed with a piece [of meat] of Lesser Holy Things or a piece [of meat] of Lesser Holy Things that are consumed on one day as for example, the peace-offerings of thanksgiving with pieces [of meat] of Lesser Holy Things that are consumed for two days and one night, which are the rest of the peace-offerings, in this, the Sages agree with Rabbi Shimon that they should consume in accordance with the rules of the more stringent of them, for what else should he do?



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:1)


**אֵבְרֵי חַטָּאת.** שֶׁבְּשָׂרָהּ נֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֲנִים:


אברי חטאת – whose flesh/meat is consumed by the Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:2)


**שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ בְאֵבְרֵי עוֹלָה.** שֶׁכֻּלָּהּ כָּלִיל:


שנתערבו באברי עולה – that is entirely burned up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:3)


**יִתֵּן לְמַעְלָן.** יִתֵּן אֵבְרֵי הַתַּעֲרֹבוֹת כֻּלָּן עַל הָעֵצִים. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא ב) כִּי כָל שְׂאֹר וְכָל דְּבַשׁ לֹא תַקְטִירוּ מִמֶּנּוּ אִשֶּׁה לַה', וְהָכִי מַשְׁמָעוּתָא דִקְרָא, כִּי כָל שְׂאֹר וְכָל דְּבַשׁ לֹא תַקְטִירוּ, וְלֹא תַקְטִירוּ כָּל שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ אִשֶּׁה לַה', דְּהַיְנוּ הַשִּׁירַיִם שֶׁל קָרְבָּן. קָרְבָּן רֵאשִׁית תַּקְרִיבוּ מִן הַשְּׂאֹר וְהַדְּבַשׁ לַה', שְׁתֵּי לֶחֶם מִן הַשְּׂאֹר וּדְבַשׁ מִן הַבִּכּוּרִים מִפְּרִי הָעֵץ שֶׁהֵם תְּאֵנִים וּתְמָרִים. וְאֶל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ דָּרְשִׁינַן לֵיהּ בֵּין אַשְּׂאֹר וּדְבַשׁ בֵּין אַשִּׁירַיִם, לְרֵיחַ נִיחוֹחַ אִי אַתָּה מַעֲלֶה אֲבָל אַתָּה מַעֲלֶה לְשֵׁם עֵצִים. וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָאָמַר וְרוֹאֶה אֲנִי אֶת בְּשַׂר חַטָּאת כְּאִלּוּ הֵן עֵצִים:


יתן למעלן – he should place the limbs of the entire mixture on the wood. And the reason of Rabbi Eliezer is as Scripture states (Leviticus 2:11): “for no leaven or honey may be turned into smoke as a gift to the LORD,” and this is the logical implication of the Biblical verse (ibid.,): “for no leaven or honey may be turned into smoke,” and you should not turn into smoke anything that from which is a fire-offering to God, which is the residue of the sacrifice. “You may bring them to the LORD as an offering of choice products” (Leviticus 2:12), from the leaven and honey to God, the two loaves from the leaven and the honey from the First Fruits from the fruit of the trees which are figs and dates. [And the words], “upon the altar” (ibid.), we expound it, whether it is the leaven or the honey or whether it is the residue, for a pleasing odor [to God] you do not offer them up, but you do offer them up for the sake of wood, and this is what is stated, “and I regard the meat of the sin-offering [which is] on top [of the fires] as if it were wood.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:4:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים תְּעֻבַּר צוּרָתָן.** כְּלוֹמַר יַנִּיחַ אוֹתָן עַד שֶׁיִּפָּסְלוּ וְיֵצְאוּ לְבֵית הַשְּׂרֵפָה כִּשְׁאָר פְּסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁים, וְאֵינוֹ מַעֲלָן לַמִּזְבֵּחַ, דְּהָא עָבַר מִשּׁוּם כָּל שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ לָאִשִּׁים הֲרֵי הוּא בְּבַל תַּקְטִירוּ, וְחַטָּאת דְּאֵמוּרָיו קְרֵבִים לָאִשִּׁים הֲרֵי הַבָּשָׂר בְּבַל תַּקְטִירוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים תעובר צורתן – that is to say, he should leave them until they become disqualified and they would go out to the place of burning [as remnant] like the rest of the consecrated animals that are disqualified, and he doesn’t offer them up on the altar, for he has transgressed because of all that from it were to be burnt-offerings (Leviticus 2:11): “for no heaven or honey may be turned into smoke as a gift to the LORD,” – which he violated not turning it into smoke, but the sin-offering and the portions of the offering placed upon the altar are offered as burnt-offerings, the flesh comes under the ban against not turning it into smoke. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:5:1)


**אֵבָרִים בְּאֶבָרֵי בַעֲלֵי מוּמִים.** אֵבָרִים כְּשֵׁרִים שֶׁנִּתְעָרַב בָּהֶן אֵבָר אֶחָד שֶׁל בַּעֲלֵי מוּמִין, וּבָא כֹהֵן שֶׁאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ בְּתַעֲרָבְתָּן וְהִקְרִיב אֶחָד מִן הָרָאשִׁין, יַקְרִיבוּ כָל הָרָאשִׁין לְכַתְּחִלָּה, דְּתָלִינַן אִסּוּרָא בְּהַהוּא דְּקָרֵב, וְהָנָךְ כֻּלְּהוּ כְּשֵׁרִים נִינְהוּ. וּבַגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דְּלֹא שָׁרֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אֶלָּא לְהַקְרִיבָן שְׁנַיִם שְׁנַיִם, דְּוַדַּאי חַד מֵהָנָךְ זוּג דְּהֶתֵּרָא הוּא וְנִתְּנָה הַקְרָבָה זוֹ לִקָּרֵב מִשּׁוּם הַהוּא דְהֶתֵּר. וְאִי מִשּׁוּם הַהוּא דִּסְפֵק אִסּוּר אִיכָּא לְמִתְלְיֵהּ בְּאוֹתוֹ שֶׁקָּרַב תְּחִלָּה קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּמְלַךְ. אֲבָל אֶחָד אֶחָד שֶׁמָּא לֹא נִתְּנָה הַקְרָבָה זוֹ לִקָּרֵב אֲפִלּוּ לְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דִּלְמָא הַאי דְאִסּוּרָא הוּא:


אברים באברי בעלי מומים – kosher/fit limbs that were mixed with one limb of a blemished beast, and a Kohen comes and he doesn’t know of their mixture and he offered one of the heads, he should offer all of the heads ab initio, for we assign the prohibited one with that which was offered, but all of these are kosher. And in the Gemara (Tractate Zevakhim 77b) it explains that Rabbi Eliezer did not permit other than to offer them in twos [but not singly], for certainly one from those pairs is permitted and this sacrifice was to be offered because of that one that is permissible. But if it is because that one where there is doubtful prohibition, there would be a doubt with that which was offered first before he changed his mind. But “one by one” lest he didn’t give that sacrifice to be made even according to Rabbi Eliezer, for perhaps this one was forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:5:2)


**חוּץ מֵאַחַד.** חוּץ מִזּוּג אֶחָד. הָכִי מְפָרֵשׁ לֵיהּ בַּגְּמָרָא. דִּבְחַד גְּרֵידָא מוֹדֶה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּלֹא נִתְּנָה הַקְרָבָה זוֹ לִקָּרֵב, כְּדַאֲמָרַן:


חוץ מאחד – except for one pair. And this is explained in the Gemara, that with one pair alone [of heads or legs], Rabbi Eliezer admits that this sacrifice was not given to be offered, as we have stated.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:1)


**אִם יֶשׁ בּוֹ מַרְאֵה דָם כָּשֵׁר.** וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁנָּפְלוּ הַמַּיִם לְתוֹךְ דָּם שֶׁל קָדָשִׁים. אֲבָל אִם נָפַל דָּם שֶׁל קָדָשִׁים לְתוֹךְ מַיִם, אָמְרִינַן קַמָּא קַמָּא בָּטִיל, וַאֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ בּוֹ מַרְאֵה דָם פָּסוּל:


אם יש בו מראה דם כשר – and these words [apply] when the water fell in to the blood of Holy Things [and it has the appearance of blood - which can be sprinkled on the altar]. But if the blood of Holy Things fell into water, we state, each drop is nullified as it falls (Talmud Zevakhim 78a), and even if it has the appearance of blood, it is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:2)


**נִתְעָרֵב בְּיַיִן.** שֶׁהוּא אָדוֹם:


נתערב ביין – which is red.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:3)


**רוֹאִין אוֹתוֹ כְּאִלּוּ הוּא מָיִם.** וְאִם הָיָה דָם זֶה נִכָּר בְּאוֹתָן הַמַּיִם, כָּשֵׁר:


רואין אותו כאילו הוא מים – but if this blood is recognized in those of the water, it is kosher/fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:4)


**בְּדַם בְּהֵמָה.** שֶׁל חֻלִּין:


בדם בהמה – [the blood of an animal] that is unconsecrated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:5)


**רוֹאִין אוֹתוֹ.** דָּם שֶׁל חֻלִּין כְּאִלּוּ הוּא מַיִם, וְאִם הָיָה מַרְאֵה אַדְמוּמִית שֶׁל דָּם הַכָּשֵׁר נִכָּר בָּהֶם, כָּשֵׁר, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַדָּם הַפָּסוּל מְרֻבָּה עָלָיו הַרְבֵּה:


רואין אותו – the blood of an unconsecrated [animal] as if it is water, and if it had the appearance of the reddish color of kosher blood that is recognized by them, it is kosher, and even though the disqualified blood is greater than it by a lot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:6:6)


**אֵין דָּם מְבַטֵּל דָּם.** דְּמִין בְּמִינוֹ לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה לֹא בָטִיל, וַאֲפִלּוּ טִפָּה לְתוֹךְ כְּלִי גָדוֹל, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאִלּוּ הָיָה הַדָּם מַיִם אֵין הַדָּם הַכָּשֵׁר נִכָּר בָּהֶם, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי כָּשֵׁר לִזְרִיקָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אין דם מבטל דם – according to Rabbi Yehuda, one type mixed with the same type is not annulled, and even a drop within a large vessel. Even though if the blood was water, the kosher blood is not recognized in them, and even so, it is kosher/fit for sprinkling. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:7:1)


**נִתְעָרֵב בְּדַם הַפְּסוּלִים.** כְּגוֹן בְּדָם שֶׁנִּשְׁחַט לְחוּץ לִזְמַנּוֹ:


נתערב בדם הפסולים – as for example in blood that was ritually slaughtered outside of its proper time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:7:2)


**יִשָּׁפֵךְ לָאַמָּה.** לְאַמַּת הַמַּיִם הָעוֹבֶרֶת בָּעֲזָרָה וְיוֹרֶדֶת לְנַחַל קִדְרוֹן. וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן רוֹאִים אוֹתוֹ כְּאִלּוּ הוּא מַיִם וְאִם דָּם הַכָּשֵׁר נִכָּר בָּהֶם יִזָּרֵק, דְּגָזְרִינַן דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאַכְשׁוֹרֵי דַם פְּסוּלִים בְּעֵינַיְהוּ. וְכֵן דַּם הַתַּמְצִית. דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא דַּם הַתַּמְצִית לֹא חֲזִי לְכַפָּרָה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יז) כִּי נֶפֶשׁ הַבָּשָׂר בַּדָּם הִוא וַאֲנִי נְתַתִּיו לָכֶם עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ לְכַפֵּר. דָּם שֶׁהַנֶּפֶשׁ יוֹצְאָה בוֹ מְכַפֵּר, וְשֶׁאֵין הַנֶּפֶשׁ יוֹצְאָה בוֹ אֵינוֹ מְכַפֵּר. וְגָזְרִינַן עַל דַּם הַנֶּפֶשׁ שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בְּדַם הַתַּמְצִית, דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאַכְשׁוֹרֵי בַּזְּרִיקָה דַּם הַתַּמְצִית בְּעֵינֵיהּ:


ישפך לאמה – to the river-arm/sewer that passes in the Temple courtyard and goes down to the Kidron ravine/river-bed. And we don’t state that we view it as if it is water, but if the kosher blood is recognized in them, he would sprinkle it, for we make the decree that perhaps he would come to make fit blood that is disqualified in their eyes. And similarly, the last blood that is oozing out of a vein. For from the Torah, the last blood that is oozing out of a vein is not appropriate for atonement. As it is written (Leviticus 17:11): “For the life of the flesh is in the blood, and I have assigned it to you for making expiation for your lives upon the altar.” The life blood that leaves through it atones, but when the soul doesn’t leave, it does not atone. And we make the decree on the life blood that was mixed with the last blood that is oozing out of a vein, for perhaps it comes to be made to be fit through the sprinkling of the last blood that is oozing out of a vein in his eyes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:7:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַכְשִׁיר.** לְכַתְּחִלָּה בְּדַם הַתַּמְצִית שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב עִם דַּם הַנֶּפֶשׁ. דְּסָבַר אֵין דַּם הַתַּמְצִית שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה מָצוּי לִהְיוֹת רַבֶּה עַל דַּם הַנֶּפֶשׁ, הִלְכָּךְ אַמַּאי נִגְזוֹר, הֲוָה לֵיהּ כְּנִתְעָרֵב דַּם הַחֻלִּין בְּדַם הַקֳּדָשִׁים דְּלֹא גָזְרִינַן בֵּיהּ מִידֵי. וּכְשֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בְּדַם פְּסוּלִין דְּמִלְּתָא דִשְׁכִיחָא, לֹא פָּלֵיג רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר פְּעָמִים שֶׁדַּם הַתַּמְצִית רַבֶּה עַל דַּם הַנֶּפֶשׁ, הִלְכָּךְ גָּזַר הֵיכָא דְּלֹא רַבָּה אַטּוּ הֵיכָא דְרַבֶּה. וּמִיהוּ הֵיכָא דְּלֹא נִמְלַךְ הַכֹּהֵן בְּבֵית דִּין אִם יִתֵּן מִתַעֲרֹבֶת זוֹ אִם לָאו וְנָתַן מֵאֵלָיו, מוֹדֶה תַנָּא קַמָּא דְכָשֵׁר, דִּגְזֵרָה בְעָלְמָא הִיא וּבְדִיעֲבַד כָּשֵׁר. וְכִי אָתֵי לְאִמְּלוֹכֵי, אָמְרִינַן לֵיהּ לְשׁוֹפְכוֹ לָאַמָּה מִשּׁוּם גְּזֵרָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


רבי אליעזר מכשיר – ab initio with the last blood that is oozing out of a vein that was mixed with the blood of the soul. For he holds that the last blood that is oozing out of a vein of an animal is found to be greater than the life blood, therefore, why do we make a decree, it is to for him like when the blood of an unconsecrated animal is mixed with the blood of Holy Things, that we don’t decree anything regarding it. For when it is mixed with disqualified blood, which is a matter that is frequent, Rabbi Eliezer does not dispute. But the first Tanna/teacher holds that sometimes when the last blood that is oozing out of a vein is greater than the life blood. Therefore, he decreed where it was not greater, on account of the where it is greater. But however, where the Kohen took counsel with the Jewish court if he should give this mixture or if not, and he gave of his own accord, the first Tanna/teacher admits that it is kosher/appropriate, for it is mere decree and de facto it is kosher. But if he came to change his mind, we tell him that to pour it into the sewer because of the decree. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:8:1)


**דַּם תְּמִימִים שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בְּדַם בַּעֲלֵי מוּמִין יִשָּׁפֵךְ לָאַמָּה.** וּבְהָא לֹא פָלֵיג רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, דְּעָבַר בִּזְרִיקָתָן מִשּׁוּם לֹא תַקְרִיבוּ:


דם תמימים שנתערב בדם בעלי מומין ישפך לאמה – and in this, Rabbi Eliezer does not dispute, for he transgressed through their sprinkling because of (Leviticus 22:20): “You shall not offer any that has a defect.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:8:2)


**נִתְעָרֵב כּוֹס בְּכוֹסוֹת.** כּוֹס דָּם שֶׁל בַּעַל מוּם בְּכוֹסוֹת שֶׁל דָּם כָּשֵׁר, וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אֵיזֶהוּ כּוֹס שֶׁל דַּם בַּעַל מוּם:
