נתער בכוס שבכוסות – a cup of blood of an animal with a blemish with cups of kosher blood, and it is not known which cup of blood is that of a blemished animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:8:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר אִם קָרַב אֶחָד מֵהֶם כוּ'.** כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ בְּאֵבָרִים וְאֵבְרֵי בַעֲלֵי מוּמִין, כָּךְ נֶחְלְקוּ בְּכוֹסוֹת, וְּכִדְפָרִישְׁנָא לְעֵיל. וּצְרִיכָא לְאַפְלוּגֵי בְּתַרְוַיְהוּ, דְּאִי אַשְׁמְעִינַן בְּאֵבָרִים, בְּהָא קָאָמַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר יִקְרְבוּ מִשּׁוּם דְּאִתְעָבִידָא כַפָּרָתָן בְּהֶכְשֵׁר, דְּהַיְנוּ זְרִיקַת דָּמָן שֶׁהִיא עִקַּר כַּפָּרָה, אֲבָל גַּבֵּי כוֹס בְּכוֹסוֹת דְּעִקַּר כַּפָּרָה בְּקִלְקוּל, אֵימָא לֹא, צְרִיכָא:


ר' אליעזר אומר אם קרב אחד מהם כו' – in the manner that they disputed with the limbs [of kosher animals] and the limbs of animals with blemishes (see Mishnah 5 of this chapter), so they also disputed with regard to cups, as it is explained above. And it is necessary to dispute on both of them, for if we were to teach [only] regarding limbs, it is in this that Rabbi Eliezer states that they should be offered because their expiation is effected with the kosher [blood], that is, with the sprinkling of their blood which is the essence of the atonement, but regarding a cup [of one kind of blood] that was mixed with cups [of another kind of blood], that the essence of expiation is through deterioration, I would say, no. so both are necessary.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:1)


**הַנִּתָּנִין לְמַטָּה.** מִחוּט הַסִּקְרָא. כְּגוֹן דַּם עוֹלָה וְאָשָׁם וּשְׁלָמִים וּבְכוֹר וּפֶסַח וּמַעֲשֵׂר, שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ בְּדַם חַטָּאת שֶׁהוּא נִתַּן לְמַעְלָה:


הנתנין למטה – from the red line. As, for example, blood of the burnt-offering and the guilt-offering and the peace-offering and the firstling and the Passover offering and the tithe [which are offered below the red line], that were mixed up with the blood of the sin-offering which was sprinkled above [the red line].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:2)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יִתֵּן לְמַעְלָה.** מַתְּנוֹת חַטָּאת. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדַּם הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים מְעֹרָב בּוֹ. הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ מִתְכַּוֵּן לָתֵת הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים לְמַעְלָה רוֹאֶה אֲנִי אוֹתָם כְּאִלּוּ הֵם מַיִם. וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁמִּצְוָה לְהַקְדִּים עֶלְיוֹנִים לְתַחְתּוֹנִים, שֶׁכָּל הַחַטָּאוֹת קוֹדְמוֹת לָעוֹלוֹת, הִלְכָּךְ נוֹתֵן לְמַעְלָה תְּחִלָּה וְאַחַר כָּךְ לְמַטָּה, וּנְתִינַת מַטָּה תַּעֲלֶה לוֹ לִשְׁפִיכַת שִׁירַיִם שֶׁל חַטָּאת וּלְהַתְחָלַת מַתְּנוֹת עוֹלָה:


רבי אליעזר אומר יתן למעלה – gifts of sin-offerings. But even though that the blood of the lower sacrifices (i.e., sprinkled below the red line) ae mixed with it. For since he doesn’t intend to place/sprinkle the lower ones above, I see them as if they are water. And because it is a Mitzvah to advance the upper ones over the lower ones, for all of the sin-offerings precede the burnt-offerings, therefore, he sprinkles above first and afterward the lower ones, and in sprinkling the lower ones, it will count for him the spilling of the residue of the sin-offering and beginning of the gifts of the burnt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:3)


**יִשָּׁפְכוּ לָאַמָּה.** דְּלֵית לְהוּ רוֹאִים:


ישפכו לאמה – they don’t accept “seeing” (i.e., as in viewing the blood from the sacrifices sprinkled below the red line as water).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:9:4)


**וְאִם לֹא נִמְלַךְ וְנָתַן כָּשֵׁר.** דְּהָא נִתַּן מִמֶּנּוּ לְמַעְלָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ואם לא נמלך ונתן כשר – for he sprinkled from it above. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:1)


**הַנִּתָּנִין בְּמַתָּנָה אַחַת.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ כְּגוֹן כּוֹס דַּם בְּכוֹר שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בְּכוֹס דַּם מַעֲשֵׂר:


הניתנין במתנה אחת – In the Gemara (Tractate Zevakhim 81a) as for example, cup of the blood of a firstling that was mixed in a cup of the blood of a tithe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:2)


**יִנָּתְנוּ מַתָּנָה אֶחָת.** מִכָּל כּוֹס וְכוֹס יִתֵּן מַתָּנָה אַחַת מִכּוֹס זֶה וּמַתָּנָה אַחַת מִכּוֹס זֶה:


ינתנו מתנה אחת – from each cup individual cup he gives one gift [of sprinkling] from this cup and another gift [of sprinkling] from that cup.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:3)


**מַתַּן אַרְבַּע בְּמַתַּן אַרְבַּע.** כְּגוֹן דַּם עוֹלָה בְּדַם שְׁלָמִים אוֹ בְּדַם אָשָׁם, שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם טְעוּנִים שְׁתֵּי מַתָּנוֹת שֶׁהֵן אַרְבַּע וּשְׁנֵיהֶם תַּחְתּוֹנִים:


מתן ארבע במתן ארבע – as, for example, the blood of a burnt-offering with the blood of a peace-offering or the blood of a guilt-offering, for both of them require two gifts which are four and both of them are [sprinkled] below [the red line].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:4)


**מַתַּן אַרְבַּע בְּמַתַּן אֶחָד.** כְּגוֹן עוֹלָה בִּבְכוֹר שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם תַּחְתּוֹנִים:


מתן ארבע במתן אחד = as, for example, the [blood of] a burnt-offering with [the blood of] a firstling that both of them are [sprinkled] below [the red line].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יִנָּתְנוּ בְמַתַּן אַרְבַּע.** וְרוֹאֶה אֲנִי אֶת מַתָּנוֹת הַיְתֵרוֹת הַנִּתָּנוֹת מִדַּם הַבְּכוֹר כְּאִלּוּ הֵם מַיִם. וְאֵין זֶה בַּל תּוֹסִיף:


רא"א ינתנו במתן ארבע – and I see the extra gifts that are sprinkled from the blood of the firstling as if they are water, and this is not [a violation of] “do not add” (i.e., Deuteronomy 13:1: “[Be careful to observe only that which I enjoin upon you:] neither add to it [nor take away from it.”].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:6)


**בְּמַתָּנָה אֶחָת.** שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲבֹר עַל בַּל תּוֹסִיף. וִידֵי עוֹלָה יָצָא, דְּכָל הַנִּתָּנִים עַל מִזְבֵּחַ הַחִיצוֹן שֶׁנְּתָנָן בְּמַתָּנָה אַחַת כִּפֵּר:


במתנה אחת so that he will not transgress on “do not add” (see above, Deuteronomy 13:1), but regarding the burnt-offering, he has fulfilled it, for all that are given/sprinkled on the outer altar when they are given/sprinkled in one gift, atones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:10:7)


**וְלֹא עָשִׂיתָ מַעֲשֶׂה בְיָדֶךָ.** וְאֵינוֹ דוֹמֶה עוֹשֶׂה מַעֲשֶׂה, לְיוֹשֵׁב וְאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה וְאִסּוּר בָּא מֵאֵלָיו. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ולא עשית מעשה בידך – and it is not similar to someone doing an action, to sitting and not doing and the prohibition comes of its own accord. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:1)


**הַנִּתָּנִין בִּפְנִים כוּ'‏ יִשָּׁפְכוּ לָאַמָּה.** דְּלֹא אָמְרִינַן רוֹאִים, וְאָסוּר לְשַׁנּוֹת אֶת אֵלּוּ מִפְּנֵי הֶכְשֵׁרָן שֶׁל אֵלּוּ. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּסָבַר אָמְרִינַן רוֹאִין, לֹא פָלֵיג הָכָא. דְּהֵיכִי לַעֲבִיד, נֵיתִיב לַחוּץ וַהֲדַר לִפְנִים, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁמִּצְוָה לְהַקְדִּים עֶלְיוֹנִים לְתַחְתּוֹנִים כָּךְ מִצְוָה לְהַקְדִּים פְּנִימִי לְחִיצוֹן, דִּפְנִימִי חָשׁוּב. נֵיתִיב בִּפְנִים וַהֲדַר נֵיתִיב בַּחוּץ, כֵּיוָן דְּאִיכָּא חַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם שֶׁאִם נִתְעָרֵב דָּמָם בַּדָּמִים הַפְּנִימִים לֹא מָצֵי לְמֵימַר יִתֵּן בִּפְנִים וְאַחַר כָּךְ יִתֵּן בַּחוּץ מִשּׁוּם דְּמִפַּסְלֵי חִיצוֹנִים, לֹא פְסִיקָא לֵיהּ. וְאִם תֹּאמַר לִפְסְלִינְהוּ לַחִיצוֹנִים כְּדֵי לְהַכְשִׁיר פְּנִימִים. לְמִפְסְלִינְהוּ בְּיָדַיִם לֹא קָאָמַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, וּמוּטָב לְהַמְתִּין עַד שְׁקִיעַת הַחַמָּה וְיִפָּסְלוּ מֵאֲלֵיהֶן וְיִשָּׁפְכוּ לָאַמָּה:


הניתנין בפנים כו' ישפכו לאמה – for we don’t say, “we see,” (see Mishnah 9 of this chapter) and it is prohibited to change these because of the fitness/acceptability of those. But Rabbi Eliezer who held that we state, “we see,” does not dispute here. For how should we proceed? Let us sprinkle outside and then afterwards inside, for just as it is a commandment to have the upper parts [of the red line] precede the lower parts, so it is a Mitzvah to have that which is offered inside precede that which is offered outside, for the inside is crucial. Let us sprinkle inside and afterwards sprinkle outside, since there is [the blood] of a sin-offering and a guilt-offering, for if it their blood was mixed with the inner blood, one is not able to state that he should give/sprinkle inside and afterwards, give/sprinkle outside, because it invalidates [the blood sprinkled outside, it was not decided with him (i.e., he could not state it absolutely). But if you would say that we should invalidate the [blood sprinkled] for the outside [sacrifices] in order to validate the [blood sprinkled] for the inner sacrifices, Rabbi Eliezer doe not state that we would invalidate them with our hands, and it is better to wait until sunset so that they will be invalidated of their own accord and they would be poured into the sewer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:2)


**נָתַן בַּחוּץ.** כֹּהֵן שֶׁלֹּא נִמְלַךְ וְנָתַן מִתַּעֲרֹבֶת זוֹ בַּחוּץ, כָּשֵׁר:


נתן בחוץ – a Kohen who did not consult [with the Jewish court] and gave/sprinkled from this mixture outside [the altar], it is kosher/fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:3)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא פוֹסֵל.** הַחִיצוֹן. שֶׁכָּל דָּמִים הַחִיצוֹנִים שֶׁנִּכְנְסוּ לִפְנִים נִפְסְלוּ:


רבי עקיבא פוסל החיצון – for all the outer blood that entered inside [the altar] were invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:11:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים מַכְשִׁירִים.** בְּכָל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת, חוּץ מֵחַטָּאת הַחִיצוֹנָה דְּמִפַּסְלָא מִשּׁוּם וְכָל חַטָּאת אֲשֶׁר יוּבָא כוּ'. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מכשירין – for all of the sacrifices except for the outside sin-offering that is invalidated because of (Leviticus 6:23): “But no sin offering may be eaten from which any blood is brought [into the Tent of Meeting for expiation in the sanctuary; any such shall be consumed in fire].” And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:1)


**יָצָא אַחַד מֵהֶן לַחוּץ.** חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה:


יצא אחד מהן לחוץ – outside of the Temple courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:2)


**הַפְּנִימִי כָּשֵׁר.** וְנוֹתֵן מִמֶּנּוּ מַתְּנוֹתָיו וְהַקָּרְבָּן כָּשֵׁר:


הפנימי כשר – and he gives from it his gifts and the sacrifice is kosher/fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים פּוֹסְלִין.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ו) אֲשֶׁר יוּבָא מִדָּמָהּ, וַאֲפִלּוּ מִקְצָת דָּמָהּ, פָּסוּל, מִדְּלֹא כְתִיב אֶת דָּמָהּ:


וחכמים פוסלין – as it is written (Leviticus 6:23): “[But no sin offering may be eaten] from which any blood is brought [into the Tent of Meeting for expiation in the sanctuary],” and even part of its blood, it is invalid, for it is not written, “its blood.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:4)


**וּמָה אִם בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהַמַּחֲשָׁבָה פוֹסֶלֶת בַּחוּץ.** כְּלוֹמַר, וּמַה חוּץ, שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם שֶׁהַמַּחֲשָׁבָה פוֹסֶלֶת בּוֹ, שֶׁאִם חָשַׁב בַּשְּׁחִיטָה עַל מְנָת לִזְרֹק דָּמָהּ לַחוּץ, פְּסוּלָה:


ומה אם במקום שהמחשבה פוסלת בחוץ (see Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 2, Mishnah 2 and the beginning of this Mishnah) – meaning to say, that just as the outside [of the Temple courtyard], which is a place where the intention invalidates it, if he intended to perform the ritual slaughter on the condition to sprinkle its blood outside [the Temple courtyard], it is invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:5)


**לֹא עָשָׂה.** אֵצֶל מוֹצִיא מִקְצָת דָּמָהּ לַחוּץ אֶת הַמְשֻׁיָּר בִּפְנִים כַּיּוֹצֵא, כִּדְאַמְרִיתוּ הַפְּנִימִי כָּשֵׁר:


לא עשה – regarding removing part of the blood outside that which is that what remains inside like it goes forth, as you said that the inner [sprinkling] was kosher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:6)


**מְקוֹם שֶׁאֵין הַמַּחֲשָׁבָה פוֹסֶלֶת בִּפְנִים.** כְּלוֹמַר הֵיכָל שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין מַחֲשָׁבָה פוֹסֶלֶת בּוֹ, שֶׁאִם שָׁחַט עַל מְנָת לִתֵּן אֶת הַנִּתָּנִים בַּחוּץ בִּפְנִים, כָּשֵׁר, אֵינוֹ דִין כוּ'. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי. וְדַוְקָא בְּדָם הוּא דְאָמְרִינַן דְּאִם יָצָא חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה אוֹ נִכְנַס בִּפְנִים לְהַזּוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ בַּהֵיכָל נִפְסַל. אֲבָל בְּשַׂר קָדָשִׁים נְהִי דְּאִם יָצָא לַחוּץ נִפְסַל וְאָסוּר בַּאֲכִילָה, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) וּבָשָׂר בַּשָּׂדֶה טְרֵפָה, בָּשָׂר שֶׁיָּצָא חוּץ לִמְחִצָּתוֹ, דְּהַיְנוּ קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה וְקָדָשִׁים קַלִּים מִחוּץ לָעִיר, נַעֲשָׂה כְאִלּוּ הוּא בָּשָׂר בַּשָּׂדֶה וַהֲרֵי הוּא טְרֵפָה וְלֹא תֹאכְלוּ. מִכָּל מָקוֹם אִם נִכְנַס לִפְנִים אֶל תּוֹךְ הַהֵיכָל לֹא נִפְסַל בִּשְׁבִיל כֵּן וּמֻתָּר בַּאֲכִילָה, דְּאָמַר קְרָא אֲשֶׁר יוּבָא מִדָּמָהּ לֹא תֹאכַל, מִדָּמָהּ וְלֹא מִבְּשָׂרָה:


מקום שאין המחשבה פוסלת – that is to say the hall containing the golden altar which is a place where intention does not render [the rite] invalid, that if he ritually slaughtered in order to give the pieces [of the sacrifice] outside, inside it is kosher/fit, does it not follow, etc. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi Haglili. And especially regarding the blood in which we stated that if he went outside of the Temple courtyard or entered inside to sprinkle from it in the hall containing the golden altar, it was invalidated, but the flesh/meat of Holy Things, assuming that if he went outside [the Temple courtyard], it was invalidated and consuming it is prohibited, as it is written (Exodus 22:30): “[You shall be holy people to Me:] you must not eat flesh torn by beasts in the field; [you shall cast it to the dogs],” meat that went outside its compartment/partition , that is, the Holiest of the Holy Things outside of the Temple courtyard and the Lesser Holy Things outside of the city, it becomes as if it is flesh in the field, and it is considered “torn” and you cannot consume it. Nevertheless, if he entered inside into the midst of the hall containing the golden altar, it is not invalidated because of this and its consumption is permitted, as Scripture states (Leviticus 6:23): “[But no purification offering may be eaten] from which any blood is brought [into the Tent of Meeting for expiation in the sanctuary],” from its blood, but not from its flesh [which is permitted].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:7)


**עַד שֶׁיְּכַפֵּר.** בַּהֵיכָל:


עד שיכפר – in the hall containing the golden altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:8)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה אומר כו' – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 8:12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/8:12:9)


**וְאֵין הַצִּיץ מְרַצֶּה עַל הַיּוֹצֵא.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּפְסוּלִים שֶׁעָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ, אַרְצוֹיֵי מִיהָא לֹא אַרְצוֹ, דִּכְתִיב בַּצִּיץ (שם כח) וְנָשָׂא אַהֲרֹן אֶת עֲוֹן הַקֳּדָשִׁים, וּבְפָרָשַׁת אֱמוֹר כְּתִיב כָּל אִישׁ אֲשֶׁר יִקְרַב מִכָּל זַרְעֲכֶם אֶל הַקֳּדָשִׁים וְגוֹ'‏ וְטֻמְאָתוֹ עָלָיו, מַה קָדָשִׁים הָאֲמוּרִים לְהַלָּן בְּטֻמְאָה הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר, אַף עֲוֹן קָדָשִׁים הָאֲמוּרִים בַּצִּיץ בְּטֻמְאָה הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר:


ואין הציץ מרצה על היוצא – and even though that they (i.e., the blood) went up, they did not go down, but regarding effecting acceptance however, it does not effect, as it is written regarding the frontlet (Exodus 28:38): “[It shall be on Aaron’s forehead,] that Aaron may take away any sin arising from the holy things [that the Israelites consecrate, from any of their sacred donations,]” and in the Torah portion of Emor it is written (Leviticus 22:3): “If any man among your offspring, while in a state of impurity, partakes of any sacred donation that the Israelite people may consecrate to the LORD, [that person shall be cut off from before Me],” just as the Holy Things mentioned further on, the Biblical verse speaks of their defilement, so also the sin of the Holy Things that is mentioned regarding the frontlet, the Biblical verse speaks with regard to defilement.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:1)


**הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת הָרָאוּי לוֹ.** וּפְלִיגֵי תַנָּאֵי בְּפֵרוּשָׁא דְּמַתְנִיתִין מַאי נִיהוּ הָרָאוּי לוֹ:


המזבח מקדש את הראוי לו – but the Tannaim dispute in their explanation of our Mishnah what it is that is appropriate to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר כָּל הָרָאוּי לָאִשִּׁים.** לְהִשָּׂרֵף עַל גַּבֵּי הָאֵשׁ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּפְסַל, אִם עָלָה לַמִּזְבֵּחַ קִדְּשׁוֹ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ וְנַעֲשָׂה לַחְמוֹ, וְאִם עָלָה לֹא יֵרֵד. אֲבָל דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין רָאוּי לָאֵשׁ, כְּגוֹן דָּם פָּסוּל וּנְסָכִים פְּסוּלִים, אֲפִלּוּ אִם עָלוּ יֵרְדוּ:


ר' יהושע אומר כל הראוי לשאים – to be burned on the fire, even though it was invalidated, if it went up to the altar, the altar sanctified it and it became its food, and if it went up, it should not come down. But a thing that is not appropriate for the fire, as, for example, disqualified/unfit blood and disqualified libations, if they went up, they may be taken down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:3)


**הִיא הָעֹלָה עַל מוֹקְדָה.** דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא לַמּוֹקֵד וְעָלָה, הֲרֵי הוּא בַּהֲוָיָתוֹ וְלֹא יֵרֵד. הִיא, מַשְׁמַע בַּהֲוָיָתָהּ תְּהֵא:


היא העולה על מוקדה – something that is for the hearth where the fire is maintained and it went up, it is its status/condition and should not come down, it, implies that it will be in its status/condition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:4)


**רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר כָּל הָרָאוּי לַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ דָּם וּנְסָכִים שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ, אִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ. וְאֵין לְךָ לְמַעֵט אֶלָּא דָבָר שֶׁלֹּא הוּבְרַר לְחֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל מִזְבֵּחַ מֵעוֹלָם, כְּגוֹן קְמָצִים שֶׁלֹּא קָדְשׁוּ בִּכְלִי אַחַר קְמִיצָה, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַמִּנְחָה כֻלָּהּ קָדְשָׁה בִּכְלִי אֵין זֶה חֵלֶק גָּבוֹהַּ בָּרוּר, שֶׁאֵין הַמִּנְחָה קְרֵבָה כֻלָּהּ. וּבִקְמִיצָה בְלֹא מַתַּן כְּלִי, אֵין זֶה מְבֹרָר לַמִּזְבֵּחַ, וְאֵינוֹ מִתְקַדֵּשׁ אֲפִלּוּ עָלָה עַל גַּבֵּי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


רבן גמליאל אומר כל הראוי למזבח – and even the blood and its libations that were invalidated, if they were brought up, they should not come down. But there is nothing to exclude other than that thing which was not decided ever as to its appropriate portion of the altar, as for example, the handfuls of he meal-offering which the priest takes to be put on the altar (i.e., Leviticus 6:8) that were not sanctified in a utensil after the taking of a fistful of the meal-offering, for even though that the entire meal-offering was sanctified in a utensil, this is not clearly the portion of On-High (i.e., God), for the meal-offering is not entirely offered. And with a fistful of the meal-offering without the offering of the utensil, this is not sifted/selected for the altar, and it is not sanctified even if it was brought up on to the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:5)


**אֶלָּא הַדָּם וְהַנְּסָכִים.** שֶׁהֵם רְאוּיִים לַמִּזְבֵּחַ וְאֵינָם רְאוּיִם לָאִשִּׁים:


אלא הדם והנסכים – that are appropriate for the altar, but are not appropriate for the altar-fires.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:6)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר הַזֶּבַח כָּשֵׁר וְהַנְּסָכִים פְּסוּלִים.** רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן סְבִירָא לֵיהּ בִּנְסָכִים שֶׁל זֶבַח כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וּבְדָם וּבִנְסָכִים הַבָּאִים בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָן כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. וְלֹא מִמּוֹקְדָה יָלֵיף, אֶלָּא מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב (שמות כט) כָּל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּמִּזְבֵּחַ יִקְדָּשׁ, וּכְתִיב בַּתְרֵיהּ וְזֶה אֲשֶׁר תַּעֲשֶׂה עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ כְּבָשִׂים בְּנֵי שָׁנָה תְמִימִים שְׁנַיִם לַיּוֹם עוֹלָה, אֵין הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְקַדֵּשׁ אֶלָּא דוּמְיָא דְּעוֹלָה, מָה עוֹלָה הַבָּאָה בִּגְלַל עַצְמָהּ, אַף כָּל הַבָּאִים בִּגְלַל עַצְמָן, יָצְאוּ נְסָכִים הַבָּאִים בִּגְלַל הַזֶּבַח. הִלְכָּךְ בֵּין שֶׁהַזֶּבַח כָּשֵׁר וְהַנְּסָכִים פְּסוּלִים כְּגוֹן שֶׁיָּצְאוּ אוֹ נִטְמְאוּ, בֵּין שֶׁהַזֶּבַח פָּסוּל וְהַנְּסָכִים כְּשֵׁרִים, דְּהוֹאִיל וְהֵן בָּאִים בִּגְלָלוֹ נִפְסָלִים עִמּוֹ וּבְטֵלָה תּוֹרַת הֶקְדֵּשׁ מֵהֶן:


ר"ש אומר הזבח כשר והנסכים פסולים – Rabbi Shimon holds like Rabbi Yehoshua regarding the libations of the sacrifice, and that the blood and the libations that come on their own like Rabban Gamaliel, and it is not derived from the hearth where the fire is maintained (Leviticus 6:2 – על מוקדה) but rather from a Biblical verse (Exodus 29:37): “whatever touches the altar shall become consecrated.” And it is written after It (Exodus 29:38,42): “Now this is what you shall offer upon the altar: two yearling lambs each day, regularly...a regular burnt offering throughout the generations,” the altar does not sanctify anything other than what is similar to a burnt-offering, just as a burnt-offering that comes on account of itself, so everything that comes on account of itself, excluding libations that come on account of the animal offering. Therefore, whether the animal offering is kosher and the libations are invalid, as for example, that left or were defiled, or whether the animal offering was invalid and the libations were kosher/fit, for since they come on its account, they are invalidated with tit and the law of being sanctified is nullified from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:7)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ זֶה וְזֶה פְּסוּלִים הַזֶּבַח לֹא יֵרֵד.** שֶׁהַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְקַדְּשׁוֹ:


ואפילו זה וזה פסולים הזבח לא ירד – from its sanctity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:1:8)


**וְהַנְּסָכִים יֵרֵדוּ.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין בָּאִים בִּגְלַל עַצְמָן אֵינָן דּוּמְיָא דְעוֹלָה וְאֵין מִזְבֵּחַ מְקַדְּשָׁן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


והנכסים ירדו – since they don’t come on account of themselves, they are not similar to the burnt-offering for the altar does not sanctify them. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:2:1)


**הַלָּן.** בֵּין דָּם בֵּין אֵמוּרִים שֶׁלָּנוּ לַיְלָה חוּץ לַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


הלן – whether blood or the portions of the sacrifice offered on the altar that stayed overnight outside of the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:2:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטָה בַלַּיְלָה כוּ'‏ אִם עָלְתָה תֵּרֵד.** דִּכְתִיב זֹאת תּוֹרַת הָעוֹלָה הִיא הָעוֹלָה, זֹאת הִיא הָעוֹלָה, הֲרֵי כָאן שְׁלשָׁה מִעוּטִים, מִעֵט נִשְׁחֲטָה בַלַּיְלָה וְשֶׁנִּשְׁפַּךְ דָּמָהּ וְשֶׁיָּצָא דָּמָהּ חוּץ לַקְּלָעִים, שֶׁאִם עָלְתָה תֵּרֵד:


ר' יהודה אומר שנשחטה בלילה וכו' אם עלתה תרד – as it is written (Leviticus 6:2): “This is the ritual of the burnt offering: The burnt offering itself [shall remain where it is burned upon the altar all night until morning],” there are here three exclusions: it (i.e., the verse) excluded something slaughtered at night, and that its blood was poured, and that its blood left outside of the curtains, that if it went up, it should be brought down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:2:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר לֹא תֵרֵד.** דְּדָרִישׁ זֹאת תּוֹרַת הָעוֹלָה, תּוֹרָה אַחַת לְכָל הָעוֹלִים, שֶׁאִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' שמעון אומר לא תרד – for he expounds (Leviticus 6:2): “This is the ritual of the burnt offering” - one Torah for all of those that go up, that if they went up, they should not go down [for its invalidity took place in the sanctuary]. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:3:1)


**מַכְשִׁיר בְּבַעֲלֵי מוּמִין.** דְּאִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ. וְדַוְקָא בְדוֹקִין שֶׁבָּעַיִן וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן הִכְשִׁיר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא, הוֹאִיל וְהֵן כְּשֵׁרִים לְכַתְּחִלָּה בְּעוֹפוֹת. אֲבָל בִּמְחֻסָּר אֵבָר דְּפָסוּל בְּעוֹפוֹת, לֹא:


מכשיר בבעלי מומין – that if they went up, they should not come down, and especially withered spots in the eye and things similar to them Rabbi Akiva declared valid, for since they are valid/fit ab initio in birds. But not lacking a limb that invalidates birds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:3:2)


**דּוֹחֶה הָיָה אַבָּא אֶת בַּעֲלֵי מוּמִין.** שֶׁאִם עָלוּ דּוֹחֶה הָיָה אוֹתָן כִּלְאַחַר יָד וְלֹא הָיָה מוֹרִידָן דֶּרֶךְ בִּזָּיוֹן בְּפַרְהֶסְיָא, וּבְהָא פָלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר יֵרְדוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס:


דוחה היה אבא את בעלי מומין – if they went up, he would reject them, as if doing a thing with the back of the hand (i.e., in a manner different from the usual way of doing it), but he would not bring them down in public in a disgraceful manner. And in this, he disagrees with the first Tanna/teacher [of our Mishnah) who stated that they should come down. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Hanina ben Antiginos [the adjutant High Priest].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:1)


**כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ.** אַכָּל הָנָךְ פְּסוּלִין דִּתְנַן בְּהוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ קָאֵי:


כשם שאם עלו לא ירדו – all of these are invalid as it is taught in the Mishnah regarding them that they should not go down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:2)


**כָּךְ אִם יָרְדוּ.** לְאַחַר שֶׁעָלוּ, לֹא יַעֲלוּ עוֹד:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:3)


**וְכֻלָּן שֶׁעָלוּ חַיִּים בְּרֹאשׁ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא קָאָמַר לַהּ, דְּמַכְשִׁיר בְּבַעֲלֵי מוּמִין. וּמוֹדֶה שֶׁאִם עָלוּ חַיִּים בְּרֹאשׁ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ יֵרְדוּ, שֶׁאֵין הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְקַדֵּשׁ בַּעֲלֵי מוּמִין חַיִּים:


וכולם שעלו חיים בראש המזבח (see Mishnah 2 of this chapter) – Rabbi Akiva stated this that he declares valid in the case of animals that are blemished. And he agrees that if they went up while living on the top of the altar, they should come down, for the altar does not sanctify animals with a blemish while living.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:4:4)


**עוֹלָה שֶׁעָלְתָה חַיָּה לְרֹאשׁ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ תֵּרֵד.** בְּעוֹלָה כְשֵׁרָה מַיְרֵי, וְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל. וְלֹא נָקַט לַהּ אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם סֵיפָא, לְלַמֶּדְךָ שֶׁרֹאשׁ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מָקוֹם רָאוּי לְהֶפְשֵׁט וְנִתּוּחַ הֵיכָא דְּעָבַר וּשְׁחָטָהּ בְּרֹאשׁ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


עולה שעלתה חיה לראש המזבח תרד – we are speaking of a kosher burnt-offering, and it is the words of all. But the Mishnah did not take this usage other than because of the concluding clause [of the Mishnah], to teach you that the top of the altar is an appropriate place for flaying it and dissecting/dividing it where he transgressed and slaughtered it at the top of the altar.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:1)


**וְאֵלּוּ אִם עָלוּ יֵרְדוּ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן כְּשֵׁרִים וּטְהוֹרִים. מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא שַׁיָּךְ בְּהוּ מִזְבֵּחַ כְּלָל:


ואלו אם עלו ירדו – and even if they are kosher and ritually pure. Because the altar is not connected with them at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:2)


**בְּשַׂר קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים כוּ'.** דְּלַאֲכִילָה קָיְמֵי וְלֹא לְמִזְבֵּחַ:


בשר קדשי קדשים – that for eating they exist and not for the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:3)


**וְהַקְּטֹרֶת.** לֹא רְאוּיָה הִיא לַמִּזְבֵּחַ הַחִיצוֹן:


והקטורת – it is not appropriate for the outer altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:5:4)


**הַצֶּמֶר שֶׁבְּרָאשֵׁי כְבָשִׂים.** גָּרְסִינַן. וְרֵישָׁא דְבָבָא אַחֲרִיתָא הִיא, וּבַהֲדֵי עֲצָמוֹת וְגִידִין קָתָנֵי לַהּ וּמִשְׁפָּט אֶחָד לָהֶם. הַצֶּמֶר שֶׁבְּרָאשֵׁי כְבָשִׂים, שֶׁל עוֹלָה. שֶׁהָרֹאשׁ אֵינוֹ בִכְלַל הֶפְשֵׁט וְקָרֵב עִם עוֹרוֹ, וְהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בִּשְׁחִיטַת חֻלִּין [דַּף כז:], מִנַּיִן לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הָרֹאשׁ שֶׁכְּבָר הֻתַּז בִּשְׁחִיטָה שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ בִּכְלַל הֶפְשֵׁט יֶשְׁנוֹ בִכְלַל הַקְטָרָה, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר אֶת הָרֹאשׁ. וְיֵשׁ צֶמֶר בְּגֹבַהּ רֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל כֶּבֶשׂ אֵצֶל עָרְפּוֹ, וְכֵן הַשֵּׂעָר שֶׁבִּזְקַן הַתְּיָשִׁים בִּזְמַן שֶׁהֵן מְחֻבָּרִים, כֻּלָּן עוֹלִים:


הצמר שבראשי כבשים – we have the reading. But the first clause of the gate is the last, for these bones and sinews that are taught [in this Mishnah], and it is one law for them. The wool that is at the top of sheep, is of a burnt-offering. That the head is not included in the flaying and is offered [in sacrifice] with its hide, and this is what we state regarding the ritual slaughtering of unconsecrated animals [Tractate Hullin 27b], from where do we learn to include the head which was already cut off in the slaughter, that it is not included the flaying, it exists within the burning on the altar, the inference teaches us (Leviticus 1:8): “[and Aaron’s sons, the priests, shall lay out the sections,] with the head [and the suet, on the wood that is on the fire upon the altar].” And there is wool at the height of the head of the sheep near its neck, and similar the hair that is in the beard of the he-goat/wether at the time that they are attached, all of them are burnt-offerings.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:1)


**וְכֻלָּם.** בֵּין פְּסוּלִים שֶׁעָלוּ דִּתְנַן בְּהוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ, בֵּין עֲצָמוֹת וְגִידִין שֶׁהֶעֱלָן מְחֻבָּרִים וַאֲכָלָתָם הָאֵשׁ:


וכולם – whether invalid that came up as it is taught in the Mishnah (see Mishnah 4 of this chapter) that they don’t go down, whether bones or sinews that were brought up attached and the fire consumed them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:2)


**וּפָקְעוּ מֵעַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** לָאָרֶץ. אסקלטי"ר בְּלַעַ"ז:


ופקעו מעל המזבח – to the ground. ISKALTIR in the foreign tongue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:3)


**לֹא יַחֲזִיר.** אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַחֲזִיר:


לא יחזיר – there is no need to return [them].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:4)


**וְכֵן גַּחֶלֶת שֶׁפָּקְעָה.** אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַחֲזִיר:


וכן גחלת שפקעה – there is no need to return [it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:5)


**אֵבָרִים שֶׁפָּקְעוּ כוּ'.** אִי דְאִית בְּהוּ מַמָּשׁ, שֶׁבְּשָׂרָם נִכָּר, אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר חֲצוֹת נַמִּי יַחֲזִיר, דְּלֹא הָוֵי עִכּוּל. וְאִי דְלֵית בְּהוּ מַמָּשׁ, שֶׁנִּשְׂרְפוּ כֻלָּן וְנַעֲשׂוּ גַּחֶלֶת, אֲפִלּוּ קֹדֶם חֲצוֹת לֹא יַחֲזִיר. אֶלָּא הָכָא בְּמַאי עָסְקִינַן, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּתְקַשּׁוּ מֵחֲמַת הָאֵשׁ שֶׁשָּׁלְטָה בְכֻלָּן וְנִשְׂרְפוּ וְלֹא נַעֲשׂוּ פֶּחָם אֲבָל כְּעֵצִים יְבֵשִׁים הֵם מִתּוֹכָם:


אברים שפקעו כו' – if they have substance, that their flesh is recognized, even after midnight also he should return [them], for they don’t have half-burned pieces/things attacked by fire that bounded off the altar. And if they don’t have substance, that were all burned and became burning coal, even before they became hard on account of the fire that had power over all of them and hey were burned and did not become charcoal but rather like dry wood from inside them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:6)


**קֹדֶם לַחֲצוֹת וּלְאַחַר חֲצוֹת.** יָלְפִינַן מִקְּרָא דִכְתִיב כָּל הַלַּיְלָה עַד הַבֹּקֶר, מִמַּשְׁמַע שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר כָּל הַלַּיְלָה אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁעַד הַבֹּקֶר, אֶלָּא תֵּן בֹּקֶר לְבָקְרוֹ שֶׁל לַיְלָה, שֶׁאֵין צְרִיכִין לִהְיוֹת עוֹד עַל הַמּוֹקֵד, וְאֵיזֶה בֹקֶר נוֹסַף עַל בָּקְרוֹ שֶׁל לַיְלָה שֶׁאֵין צְרִיכִין לִהְיוֹת עוֹד עַל הַמּוֹקֵד, זֶה חֲצוֹת. דְּבֹקֶר שֶׁל לַיְלָה הוּא עַמּוּד הַשַּׁחַר:


קודם לחצות ולאחר חצות – We derive it from Scripture as it is written (Leviticus 6:2): “[The burnt offering itself shall remain where it is burned upon the altar] all night until morning, [while the fire on the altar is kept going on it],” from implying that since it states, “all night/כל הלילה“I don’ know that it is until the morning, but rather, grant morning to the very break of the night, that it is not needed to be any longer on the hearth where the fire is maintained , and which addition morning is on the break of night that doesn’t need to be any longer on the hearth where the fire is maintained? This is midnight, for the break of the night is the crack of dawn (i.e., “dawn’s early light”).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:7)


**וּמוֹעֲלִין בָּהֶן.** דְּאַכַּתִּי בְּנֵי מִזְבֵּחַ נִינְהוּ:


ומועלין בהן – for they are yet part of the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:6:8)


**וְאֵין מוֹעֲלִין בָּהֶן.** דְּכֵיוָן דַּהֲווּ מְעֻכָּלִים הֲווּ לְהוּ דָבָר שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית מִצְוָתוֹ וְאֵין מוֹעֲלִים בּוֹ:


ואין מועלין בהן – because they were half-burned pieces/things attacked by fire that bounded off the altar, they are a thing that it’s command was performed and we don’t commit with it religious sacrilege.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:1)


**הַכֶּבֶשׁ מְקַדֵּשׁ.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות מ) וּמָשַׁחְתָּ אֶת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ וְגוֹ', אֶת, לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַכֶּבֶשׁ:


הכבש מקדש – as it is written (Exodus 40:10): “Then anoint the altar [of burnt offering and all its utensils to consecrate the altar, so that the altar shall be most holy].”[The word] "את" – [from the phrase: "ומשחת את המזבח"] [comes] to include the ramp.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:2)


**הַכֵּלִים מְקַדְּשִׁים.** דִּבְכֵלִים נַמִּי כְתִיב (שם ל) כָּל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶם יִקְדָּשׁ:


הכלים מקדשים – that regarding the utensils it is also written (Exodus 30:29): “whatever touches them shall be consecrated.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:3)


**כְּלֵי הַלַּח.** קְעָרוֹת וּמִזְרָקוֹת לְדָם וּלְיַיִן וּלְשֶׁמֶן:


כלי הלח – dishes and the bowls for the blood and wine and the oil [out of which the sprinkling is done].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:4)


**מִדּוֹת הַיָּבֵשׁ.** שְׁתֵּי מִדּוֹת שֶׁל יָבֵשׁ הָיוּ שָׁם, עִשָּׂרוֹן, וַחֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן:


מדות היבש – two measurements of dry were there – the Issaron (one-tenth of an Ephah) and the half-Issaron (also known as “Omer”).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/9:7:5)


**שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂין וְהֵן שְׁלֵמִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים כְּשֶׁהֵן שְׁלֵמִים. וּמִיהוּ כָּל כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת שֶׁנִּקְּבוּ אוֹ שֶׁנִּשְׁבְּרוּ אֵין מְתַקְּנִין שִׁבְרֵיהֶן אֶלָּא עוֹשִׂים אוֹתָן חֲדָשִׁים. וְכֵן סַכִּין שֶׁנִּפְגַּם אֵין מַשְׁחִיזִין אוֹתוֹ לְהָסִיר הַפְּגָם. וּבִגְדֵי כְהֻנָּה שֶׁנִּתְלַכְלְכוּ אֵין מְכַבְּסִין אוֹתָן אֶלָּא עוֹשִׂים חֲדָשִׁים וּמַנִּיחִין אֵלּוּ לְצֹרֶךְ פְּתִילוֹת שֶׁל מְנוֹרָה וְשִׂמְחַת בֵּית הַשּׁוֹאֵבָה. וְכָל כָּךְ לָמָּה, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין עֲנִיּוּת בִּמְקוֹם עֲשִׁירוּת. וּמִזְבֵּחַ שֶׁנִּפְגַּם פָּסוּל. וְכָל פְּגִימָה הַפּוֹסֶלֶת בְּסַכִּין שֶׁל שְׁחִיטָה, פּוֹסֶלֶת בַּמִּזְבֵּחַ. וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁהַמִּזְבֵּחַ פָּגוּם כָּל קָדָשִׁים הַנִּשְׁחָטִין בָּעֲזָרָה כֻּלָּם פְּסוּלִין, דִּכְתִיב (שם כ) וְזָבַחְתָּ עָלָיו אֶת עוֹלוֹתֶיךָ וְאֶת שְׁלָמֶיךָ, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר מִקְרָא כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ שֶׁהֲרֵי שְׁחִיטַת קָדָשִׁים בָּעֲזָרָה הִיא וְלֹא עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ, אֶלָּא עָלָיו, כְּלוֹמַר כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא שָׁלֵם אַתָּה זוֹבֵחַ, וְאֵין אַתָּה זוֹבֵחַ כְּשֶׁהוּא פָגוּם. וְכָל קָדָשִׁים הַנִּמְצָאִים בָּעֲזָרָה בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהַמִּזְבֵּחַ פָּגוּם אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בְכַשְׁרוּת, כֻּלָּם פְּסוּלִים. וַאֲפִלּוּ שְׁיָרֵי מְנָחוֹת אֵינָם נֶאֱכָלִים, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא י) וְאִכְלוּהָ מַצּוֹת אֵצֶל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ, מִפִּי הַשְּׁמוּעָה לָמְדוּ בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַמִּזְבֵּחַ שָׁלֵם וְלֹא בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא פָגוּם:


שהיו עושין והן שלימים – that they would do as when they were whole. But however, all of the sacred vessels that were perforated or broken, we don’t repair their broken parts but we make them anew. And similarly, a knife that became defective, we don’t sharpen it to remove the defect. And the clothing of the priesthood that became dirty we don’t wash them, but make new ones and place those for the needs of the wicks of the candelabrum/Menorah and the Rejoicing of the Water Drawing [during Sukkot]. Why all so much? Because there is no poverty in a place of wealth. But an altar that was defective is invalid. And any notch that invalidates a knife of ritual slaughtering invalidates the altar. And all the time that the altar is defective, all of the Holy Things that are ritually slaughtered in the Temple courtyard are all invalidated, as it is written (Exodus 20:21): “[Make for Me an altar of earth] and sacrifice on it your burnt offerings and your sacrifices of well-being,” and it is impossible to state this Scriptural verse as it implies, for the ritual slaughter of Holy Things [takes place] in the Temple courtyard and not on the altar, other than upon it, that is to say, whenever it is complete/whole, you sacrifice but you do not sacrifice when it is defective. And all of the Holy Things that are found in the Temple courtyard at the time that the altar is defective, even though they were ritually slaughtered in a fit manner, all of them are invalid. And even the residue of the meal-offerings are not consumed, as it is written (Leviticus 10:12): “[Moses spoke to Aaron and to his remaining sons, Eleazar and Ithamar: take the grain that is left over from the LORD’s gifts] and eat it unleavened beside the altar,” on the basis of a tradition we learned that at the time that the altar is whole/complete and not at the time when it is defective.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:1:1)


**כֹּל הַתָּדִיר מֵחֲבֵרוֹ קוֹדֶם אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב מִלְּבַד עֹלַת הַבֹּקֶר אֲשֶׁר לְעֹלַת הַתָּמִיד. מִכְּדִי כְתִיב מִלְּבַד עֹלַת הַבֹּקֶר, הָעֲשׂוּיָה כְבָר מַשְׁמַע, מִנַּהּ שָׁמְעִינַן דִּתְמִידִין קוֹדְמִין לְמוּסָפִין, אֲשֶׁר לְעֹלַת הַתָּמִיד לָמָּה לִּי, פְּשִׁיטָא דְּעֹלַת הַבֹּקֶר עוֹלַת הַתָּמִיד הִיא, אֶלָּא תָּלָה לְךָ טַעַם הַקְדָּמָתָהּ בְּתָדִירוּת כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּלְמֹד לִשְׁאָר תְּדִירִין שֶׁיַּקְדִּימוּ. דְּמִשּׁוּם הִיא גוּפָהּ לֹא הֲוָה צָרִיךְ לְפָרוֹשֵׁי:


כל התדיר מחבירו קודם את חבירו – as it is written (Numbers 28:23): “[You shall present these] in addition to the morning portion of the regular burnt-offering.” Since it is written, “in addition to the morning portion,” it implies that it was already done, from it we hear/learn that the daily offerings precede the additional/Musaf offerings; “of the regular burnt offering,” why do I need this? It is obvious that the burnt offering of the morning is the regular daily burnt offering, but rather, that it suspended for you the reason of its preceding [the Musaf offering] through its frequency in order that you will learn the rest of the frequent things that should precede. For since this is its essence, it was not necessary to explain it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:1)


**דַּם חַטָּאת קוֹדֵם לְדַם עוֹלָה.** אִם שְׁנֵיהֶם שְׁחוּטִין וְעוֹמְדִין לִזָּרֵק:


דם חטאת קודם לדם עולה – if both are slaughtered and are ready to be sprinkled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:2)


**שֶׁהוּא מְרַצֶּה.** מְכַפֵּר עַל חַיָּבֵי כְרֵתוֹת שֶׁצְּרִיכִים רִצּוּי גָדוֹל:


שהוא מרצה – it atones for those liable for [sins punishable by] extirpation that require great winning favor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:3)


**אֵבְרֵי עוֹלָה קוֹדְמִין.** בְּהַקְטָרָתָן:


אברי עולה קודמין – in their burning on the altar/rising up in smoke.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:4)


**לְאֵמוּרֵי חַטָּאת.** אִם נִזְרְקוּ דְּמֵי שְׁנֵיהֶם:


לאימורי חטאת – if the blood of both were sprinkled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:5)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵן כָּלִיל.** וְצַד רִבּוּי הוּא זֶה לַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


מפני שהן כליל – and there is a side of an extension of scope of this for the Altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:6)


**עַל אַרְבַּע קְרָנוֹת.** וְאָשָׁם שְׁתֵּי מַתָּנוֹת שֶׁהֵן אַרְבַּע וְלֹא עַל הַקְּרָנוֹת:


על ארבע קרנות – and the guilt-offering is two gifts which are four, but not on the horns [of the Altar].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:7)


**וְעַל הַיְסוֹד.** שְׁפִיכַת שִׁירַיִם וּבְאָשָׁם לֹא מָצִינוּ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ:


ועל היסוד – the pouring of he remainders . And with the guilt-offering, we did not find that it is stated regarding it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:2:8)


**מַתַּן אַרְבַּע.** שְׁתֵּי מַתָּנוֹת שֶׁהֵן אַרְבַּע, וּבְכוֹר אֵינוֹ טָעוּן אֶלָּא מַתָּנָה אַחַת, וְאֵינוֹ טָעוּן סְמִיכָה וּנְסָכִים וְלֹא תְנוּפַת חָזֶה וְשׁוֹק:


מתן ארבע – two gifts which are four, but the first-born does not require other than one gift, and it does not require laying of the hands or libations and nor either the waving of the breast and foreleg/shoulder.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:3:1)


**וְנֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֲנִים.** וְהַמַּעֲשֵׂר אֵין לַכֹּהֲנִים חֵלֶק בּוֹ אֶלָּא כֻּלּוֹ נֶאֱכָל לַבְּעָלִים:


ונאכל לכהנים – but the tithe [of cattle], the Kohanim have no part in, but all of it is consumed by its owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:3:2)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מִין זֶבַח.** זְבִיחַת סַכִּין. וְעוֹף בִּמְלִיקָה. וּזְבָחִים חֲשִׁיבֵי:


מפני שהוא מין זבח – slaughtering by a knife. But birds are through pinching a bird’s head, But slaughtering are [more] important.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:3:3)


**וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּקָרְבַּן עוֹף כֻּלּוֹ קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים, אוֹ חַטָּאת אוֹ עוֹלָה, זְבָחִים יֵשׁ לָהֶן לְחֵלֶק גָּבֹהַּ שְׁנֵי דְבָרִים, דָּמוֹ וְאֵמוּרָיו, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן לְחַטַּאת הָעוֹף שֶׁאֵין לַמִּזְבֵּחַ אֶלָּא דָמָהּ, וְכֵיוָן דְּקָדְמָה לְחַטַּאת הָעוֹף, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן לְעוֹלַת הָעוֹף, דְּהָא חַטַּאת הָעוֹף קוֹדֶמֶת לְעוֹלַת הָעוֹף כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל:


ויש בו קדשי קדשים – and even though the sacrifice of a bird is all Holy of Holies, or a sin-offering or a burnt offering, sacrifices [of the tithe of cattle] have to divide for the Most High (i.e., God) two things, its blood and those portions of the sacrifice offered on the altar, which is not the case for the sin-offering of a bird, for the altar only has its blood, and since it (i.e., the tithing of cattle) comes before the sin-offering of birds, all the more so to the burnt offering of a bird, for the sin-offering of a bird precedes that of a burnt-offering of a bird, as will be explained further on (i.e., in the next Mishnah).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:4:1)


**שֶׁהֵן מִינֵי דָמִים.** וּמִינֵי דָמִים כַּפָּרָתָן מְרֻבָּה:


שהן מיני דמים (they produce blood for atonement) – and [different] kinds of blood their atonement is great.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:4:2)


**חַטַּאת הָעוֹף קוֹדֶמֶת לְעוֹלַת הָעוֹף.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ה) וְהִקְרִיב אֶת אֲשֶׁר לַחַטָּאת רִאשׁוֹנָה, בָּנָה בִּנְיַן אָב לְכָל הַחַטָּאוֹת שֶׁקּוֹדְמוֹת לָעוֹלָה, בֵּין בִּבְהֵמָה בֵּין בְּעוֹף:


חטאת העוף קודמת לעולת העוף – as it is written (Leviticus 5:8): “[He shall bring them to the priest,] who shall offer first the one for the purification offering (i.e., sin-offering), [pinching its head at the nape without severing it],” he built an analogy based upon induction/בנין אב for all sin-offerings that precedes a burnt offering whether for cattle or for birds (see Tractate Zevakhim 90a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:4:3)


**וְכֵן בְּהֶקְדֵּשָׁהּ.** כְּשֶׁהוּא מַפְרִישׁ קִנּוֹ, דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁתֵּי תוֹרִים אוֹ שְׁנֵי בְנֵי יוֹנָה, קוֹרֵא שֵׁם לַחַטָּאת תְּחִלָּה:


וכן להקדשה – when he dedicates its nest, which is two doves or two pigeons, he calls first for the sin-offering.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:1)


**קוֹדְמוֹת לָאֲשָׁמוֹת.** אִם הָיָה מְחֻיָּב חַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם וּמְבִיאָן, חַטָּאת קוֹדֶמֶת. כְּדִתְנַן שֶׁדָּמָהּ נִתַּן עַל אַרְבַּע קְרָנוֹת וְעַל הַיְסוֹד:


קודמות לאשמות – if he was liable for a sin-offering and a guilt-offering and brings them the sin-offering precedes, as it is taught in the Mishnah (Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 10, Mishnah 2): “that its blood is placed on the four on the four corners [of an altar] and on the foundation.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:2)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא בָא לְהַכְשִׁיר.** אֶת הַמְּצֹרָע לְקָדָשִׁים וּלְבִיאַת מִקְדָּשׁ. הִלְכָּךְ חֲשִׁיבוּתָא הוּא לְגַבֵּיהּ, שֶׁהַטָּהֳרָה תְלוּיָה בוֹ:


מפני שהוא בא להכשיר – [because it renders fit] the leper to Holy Things and to enter into the Sanctuary, therefore, it is of importance/value regarding him, that his spiritual purity is dependent upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:3)


**וּבָאִים בְּכֶסֶף שְׁקָלִים.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ה) בְּעֶרְכְּךָ כֶּסֶף שְׁקָלִים, בַּאֲשַׁם מְעִילוֹת, וְאָתֵי אָשָׁם תָּלוּי וַאֲשַׁם גְּזֵלוֹת בִּגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה בְּעֶרְכְּךָ בְּעֶרְכְּךָ, וַאֲשַׁם שִׁפְחָה חֲרוּפָה גָּמַר בְּאַיִל אַיִל:


ובאים בכסף שקלים – as it is written (Leviticus 5:15): “[When a person commits a trespass, being unwittingly remiss about any of the LORD’s sacred tings, he shall bring as his penalty to the LORD a ram without blemish from the flock;] convertible into payment in silver by the sanctuary weight, [as a reparation/guilt offering],” as a guilt-offering for misuse of consecrated articles (i.e., restoring its value to the Temple, pay an additional fifth, and bringing this offering) and it brings an uncertain guilt-offering (i.e., brought by one who is uncertain as to whether he committed a sin that required a sin-offering).and a guilt-offering for robbery (i.e., when he denies that he owes someone money, takes a false oath to support his claim, and later admits his guilt. To atone for his misdeed, he returns the money, pays the wronged party an additional fifth of the sum, and sacrifices this offering) as an analogy [with the words]"ערכך" "ערכך" (see Leviticus 5:15: “convertible/כערכך into payment in silver by the sanctuary weight” and Leviticus 5:18): “”He shall bring to the priest a ram without blemish from the flock, or the equivalent/בערכך, as a reparation – i.e., guilt – offering.”) and the guilt-offering for a handmaiden designated to become the wife of one selected by her master/שפחה חרופה we derive [in an analogy] by "איל" "איל" (i.e., the ram of reparation offering – as in Leviticus 19:21: “But he must bring to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, as his reparation offering to the LORD a ram/איל of reparation offering” and Leviticus 5:15: “he shall bring as his penalty to the LORD a ram/איל without blemish from the flock.”).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:5:4)


**חוּץ מֵאֲשַׁם נָזִיר וַאֲשַׁם מְצֹרָע.** דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ (במדבר ו) כֶּבֶשׂ בֶּן שְׁנָתוֹ. וּמִדְּאַיִל בֶּן שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים בִּשְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים, כֶּבֶשׂ בֶּן שָׁנָה לָאו בִּשְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים הוּא:


חוץ מאשם נזיר ואשם מצורע – as it is written concerning them (Numbers 7:14): “[As his offering to the LORD he shall present:] one male lamb in its first year, without blemish, for a burnt offering; one ewe lamb in its first year, without blemish, for a purification offering; one ram without blemish for an offering of well-being.” For since a ram of two years of age is for two Sela, a lamb one year old is not for two Sela.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:6:1)


**כָּךְ הֵם קוֹדְמִים לַאֲכִילָה.** אַכֻּלְּהוּ דִּבְנֵי אֲכִילָה קָאֵי, כְּגוֹן חַטָּאת לְאָשָׁם, וְאָשָׁם לְתוֹדָה, וְתוֹדָה לִשְׁלָמִים:


כך הם קודמים לאכילה – it refers to all of them as they are consumed, such as the sin-offering is consumed prior to the guilt-offering and the thanksgiving offering [is consumed] prior to the peace offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:6:2)


**שְׁלָמִים.** שֶׁל קָרְבַּן אֶמֶשׁ:


שלמים – of the sacrifice of last night.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:6:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וְתָדִיר וּמְקֻדָּשׁ, כְּגוֹן דַּם עוֹלַת תָּמִיד וְדַם חַטָּאת עוֹמְדִים, זֶה תָּדִיר וְזֶה מְקֻדָּשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ, דְּהָא אֲמַרַן דַּם חַטָּאת קֹדֶם לְדַם עוֹלָה מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מְרַצֶּה. הָא מִלְּתָא מִבַּעְיָא בַּגְּמָרָא וְלֹא אִפְשִׁיטָא, וְנִרְאֶה דְּתָדִיר קֹדֶם:


וחכמים אומרים כו' – And the Halakha is according to the Sages. And that which is more frequent and that which is holier, such as the blood of the burnt-offering of the daily offering and the blood of a sin-offering which endure, this is more frequent and this is more holy than it, for we state that the blood of a sin-offering precedes the blood of a burnt offering because it is procures pardon. But a thing that is raised [as a question] in the Gemara (Tractate Zevakhim 90b-91a) but is not deduced (i.e., in a “conflict” between that which is more frequent/תדיר and that which is more sacred/מקודש, which takes precedence?). And it appears that what is more “frequent” takes precedence.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:7:1)


**וּבְכֻלָּן.** בְּכָל הַנֶּאֱכָלִים:


ובכולן – with all that is consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:7:2)


**רַשָּׁאִים הַכֹּהֲנִים לְשַׁנּוֹת בַּאֲכִילָתָן.** כְּגוֹן לְאָכְלָן צְלוּיִין מְבֻשָּׁלִין וּשְׁלוּקִין. דִּבְמַתְּנוֹת כְּהֻנָּה כְּתִיב (שם יח) לְךָ נְתַתִּים לְמָשְׁחָה, לִגְדֻלָּה כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהַמְּלָכִים אוֹכְלִים צָלִי שָׁלוּק וּמְבֻשָּׁל וּבְמַטְעַמִּים וְתַבְלִין:


רשאין הכנים לשנות באכילתן – such as to eat them roasted, cooked or seethed, for regarding the gifts of the priesthood it is written (Numbers 18:8): “[the LORD spoke further to Aaron: I hereby give you charge of My gifts, all the sacred donations of the Israelites;] I grant them to you and to your sons as a perquisite, a due for all time,” for greatness, in the manner that the kings/royalty eat roasted, seethed and/or cooked and with refreshments and spices.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:7:3)


**שֶׁלֹּא יָבִיא אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה לִידֵי פְסוּל.** לְפִי שֶׁהַתַּבְלִין שֶׁבָּלְעוּ טַעַם הַקֳּדָשִׁים אִם יָבֹאוּ לִידֵי נוֹתָר הֲרֵי הֵן בְּאַזְהָרָה מִשּׁוּם טַעַם הַקֳּדָשִׁים שֶׁבָּלוּעַ בָּהֶן:


שלא יביא את התרומה לידי פסול – since the spices that absorbed the flavor of the Holy Things, if they would become remnants/left-overs (i.e., past the time when they should be consumed, they would be an explicit prohibition because of the flavor of the Holy Things that were absorbed in them.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:1)


**שֶׁהוּא מִתְחַלֵּק.** לַאֲכִילַת כֹּהֲנִים:


שהוא מתחלק – [distributed] for the consumption by the Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:2)


**מוֹתַר רְקִיקֵי מִנְחוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מִנְחַת מַאֲפֶה שֶׁבָּאָה חַלּוֹת וּרְקִיקִין. חַלּוֹת בּוֹלְלָן, וּרְקִיקִין מוֹשְׁחָן. וְאָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בְּמַסֶּכֶת מְנָחוֹת [דַּף עה] מוֹשְׁחָן כְּמִין כ"י [כַּ"ף יְוָנִית], וּשְׁאָר הַשֶּׁמֶן נֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֲנִים:


מותר רקיקי מנחת ישראל – the meal-offering of something baked from which comes loaves and wafers. Loaves that are kneaded with oil and wafers that are rubbed with oil, and Rabbi Shimon said in Tractate Menahot [75a] like the [Greek] letter KI (the Greek KAF – which is an “X”), and the rest of the oil is consumed by the Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:3)


**מוֹתַר רְקִיקֵי מִנְחַת כֹּהֲנִים.** דְּמִנְחַת כֹּהֲנִים כֻּלָּהּ כָּלִיל, הַשֶּׁמֶן שֶׁצָּף עַל גַּבָּהּ וְנוֹתָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִבְלַע בָּהּ, שׂוֹרְפִים אוֹתוֹ בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ:


מותר רקיקי מנחת כהנים – for the meal offering of the Kohanim is completely burned, the oil that floats on top of it and the remnant that is not absorbed in it, they burn it separately.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:4)


**וּמוֹתַר מִנְחַת כֹּהֵן הַמָּשִׁיחַ.** לְפִי שֶׁשַּׁמְנָהּ מְרֻבָּה, שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין לְעִשָּׂרוֹן, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהִיא אֲפוּיָה תְּחִלָּה אֵין שַׁמְנָהּ נִבְלַע בִּפְתִיתֶיהָ וְצָרִיךְ לְהַקְטִיר הַמּוֹתָר בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ:


ומותר מנחת כהן המשיח – for its oil is greater, three LOG for a tenth of an Ephah, and since it is baked first, its oil is not absorbed in its broken pieces and the remnant/remainder needs to be offered up separately.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:5)


**שֶׁאֵין מִתְנַדְּבִים שָׁמֶן.** לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יַעֲלֶה עַל דַּעְתְּךָ שֶׁיְּהֵא הַשֶּׁמֶן הַמִּתְחַלֵּק אוֹ הַנִּקְטָר נְדָבָה:


שאין מתנדבים שמן – therefore, it should not occur to you that the oil is distributed or that which is offered up as incense is a free-will offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 10:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/10:8:6)


**רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן אוֹמֵר מִתְנַדְּבִים שָׁמֶן.** בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ. וְאֵין פָּחוֹת מִלֹּג. וְנִשְׂרָף בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי טַרְפוֹן. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה שֶׁמִּתְנַדְּבִים יַיִן בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ וְאֵין מִתְנַדְּבִים פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין, וְזוֹרְקִין אוֹתוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי הָאִשִּׁים וְנִשְׂרָף שָׁם. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהוּא מְכַבֶּה אֵשׁ הַמַּעֲרָכָה וְרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר לֹא תִכְבֶּה, הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ מִתְכַּוֵּן לְכַבּוֹת שָׁרֵי, דִּכְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן קַיְמָא לָן דְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְכַּוֵּן מֻתָּר. וְלֹא הָוֵי פְּסִיק רֵישֵׁיהּ, דְּאֶפְשָׁר שֶׁתִּהְיֶה הָאֵשׁ גְּדוֹלָה וַחֲזָקָה וּתְנַצֵּחַ אֶת הַיַּיִן וְלֹא יְכַבֶּנָּה:


ר' טרפון אומר מתנדבים שמן – separately. And not less than a LOG, and it is burned separately. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Tarfon. And similarly, the Halakah is that they offer wine as a free-will offering separately, and they do not offer less than three LOG, and they toss/sprinkle it on top of the altar fires and it is burned there. But even though he puts out the fire of the pile of wood on the altar of the Temple, and the All-Merciful stated (Leviticus 6:6): “[A perpetual fire shall be kept burning on the altar,] not to go out,” since he does not intend to put it out, it is permitted, for we hold like Rabbi Shimon that a thig that is not intended is permitted, and it is not an inevitable consequence. For it is possible that the fire will be large and strong and will prevail over the wine and not be put out.



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:1)


**דַּם חַטָּאת.** טָעוּן כִּבּוּס. כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא ז) וַאֲשֶׁר יַזֶּה מִדָּמָהּ וְגוֹ'‏ תְּכַבֵּס בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ:


דם חטאת. טעון כבוס – as it is written (Leviticus 6:20): “…and if any of its blood is splattered upon a garment, you shall wash the bespattered part in the sacred precinct.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ תֵּאָכֵל.** וּבְהַהוּא עִנְיָנָא כְּתִיב:


שנאמר במקום קדוש תאכל – it is written in regard to that matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:3)


**וְאֶחָד הַפְּנִימִית.** שֶׁדָּמָן טָעוּן הַזָּיָה בִּפְנִים שֶׁאֵינָן נֶאֱכָלוֹת:


ואחד הפנימית – that their blood requires sprinkling inside [in the inner area] which are not consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:4)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר תּוֹרַת הַחַטָּאת.** בְּרֵישָׁא דְהַהוּא עִנְיָנָא כְּתִיב:


שנאמר תורה אחת לכל החטאות – it is written [in the Torah – Leviticus 6:19] at the beginning of that matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:1:5)


**תּוֹרָה אַחַת לְכָל הַחַטָּאוֹת.** וְדַוְקָא חַטַּאת בְּהֵמָה. אֲבָל דַּם חַטַּאת הָעוֹף אֵינוֹ טָעוּן כִּבּוּס, דִּבְהַהוּא עִנְיָנָא תִּשָּׁחֵט הַחַטָּאת כְּתִיב, פְּרָט לְחַטַּאת הָעוֹף שֶׁאֵינָהּ נִשְׁחֶטֶת:


תורה אחת לכל החטאות – and specifically the sin offering of cattle. But the blood of sin-offering of a bird [that splatters on the clothing of the officiating priest] does not require washing, for in that matter, it is written (Leviticus 6:17): “[This is the ritual of the purification/sin-offering: the purification offering shall be slaughtered before the LORD] at the spot where the burnt offering is slaughtered,” excluding the sin-offering of the bird which is not slaughtered.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:1)


**אֵין דָּמָהּ טָעוּן כִּבּוּס.** דִּכְתִיב אֲשֶׁר יַזֶּה מִדָּמָהּ, מִדַּם כְּשֵׁרָה וְלֹא מִדַּם פְּסוּלָה:


אין דמה טעון כבוס – as it is written (Leviticus 6:20): “and if any of its blood is splattered [upon a garment],” from blood that is fit, but not blood that is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:2)


**שְׁעַת הַכֹּשֶׁר.** לִזְרִיקָה:


שעת הכושר – for tossing/sprinkling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:3)


**שֶׁלָּנָה.** שֶׁלָּן דָּמָהּ:


שלנה – that its blood remained overnight.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:2:4)


**וְשֶׁקִּבְּלוּ פְסוּלִין.** גָּרְסִינַן. וְלֹא גָרְסִינַן וְשֶׁזָּרְקוּ, דְּהָא אֲפִלּוּ חַטָּאת כְּשֵׁרָה שֶׁנִּתַּז דָּמָהּ עַל גַּבֵּי הַבֶּגֶד לְאַחַר זְרִיקַת הַדָּם אֵינוֹ טָעוּן כִּבּוּס, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן אֲשֶׁר יַזֶּה, פְּרָט לְזֶה שֶׁכְּבָר הֻזָּה:


ושקבלו פסולין – this is our reading. But we don’t read, “and when they tossed/sprinkled [its blood], for even an appropriate sin-offering whose blood was splattered on the clothing after the sprinkling of the blood idoes not require washing, as we have stated, “and if any of [its blood] is splattered,” (Leviticus 6:20), excluding that which that had already been splattered.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:1)


**נִתַּז מִן הַצַּוָּאר.** שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה עַל הַבֶּגֶד, אֵינוֹ טָעוּן כִּבּוּס. דִּכְתִיב אֲשֶׁר יַזֶּה, לֹא אָמַרְתִּי אֶלָּא בְּרָאוּי לְהַזָּאָה:


נתז מן הצואר –[from the neck] of the animal on the clothing, it does not require washing. As it is written (Leviticus 6:20): “and if its any of [its blood] is splattered,” I didn’t say other than that that whichis appropriate for sprinkling (i.e., was received in a pot).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:2)


**מִן הַקֶּרֶן.** שֶׁל מִזְבֵּחַ:


מן הקרן – [from the horn] of the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:3)


**וּמִן הַיְסוֹד.** מִן הַשִּׁירַיִם הָרְאוּיִים לִשָּׁפֵךְ עַל הַיְסוֹד, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁפְּכוּ עֲדַיִן. דְּמֵאַחַר שֶׁנִּתַּן מַתַּן דָּמָהּ אֵין שִׁירַיִם טְעוּנִים כִּבּוּס, דִּכְתִיב אֲשֶׁר יַזֶּה, פְּרָט לָזֶה שֶׁכְּבָר הֻזָּה מִדָּמָהּ:


ומן היסוד [from the foundation] – from the remnants that are appropriate to be spilled on the foundation, and even though they were not yet spilled, for after the giving of the blood is performed, the remanans do not require washing, as it is written (Leviticus 6:20): “[you shall wash] the besplattered part,” except fro that which already had been splattered of its blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:4)


**אֶלָּא הַדָּם שֶׁנִּתְקַבֵּל בִּכְלִי.** מַה טַּעַם קָאָמַר, מַה טַּעַם נִשְׁפַּךְ עַל הָרִצְפָּה וַאֲסָפוֹ אֵין טָעוּן כִּבּוּס, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין טָעוּן כִּבּוּס אֶלָּא הַדָּם שֶׁנִּתְקַבֵּל בִּכְלִי:


אלא הדם שנתקבל בכלי – what is the reason that this is stated? For what reason that if it (i.e., the blood) is spilled on the floor and collected that it does not require washing but rather [only] the blood that was received in a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:5)


**וְרָאוּי לְהַזָּיָה.** לְמַעוֹטֵי קִבֵּל פָּחוֹת מִכְּדֵי הַזָּאָה בִּכְלִי זֶה וּפָחוֹת מִכְּדֵי הַזָּאָה בִּכְלִי זֶה וְאַחַר כָּךְ עֵרְבָן, דְּלֹא קַדְּשֵׁי וְאֵין טְעוּנִין כִּבּוּס:


וראוי להזאה – to exclude receiving less than what is needed for sprinkling in this vessel, and less than what [is needed] for sprinkling in that [other] vessel but afterwards he mixed them, they are not holy and do not require washing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:6)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא הֻפְשַׁט אֵין טָעוּן כִּבּוּס.** דִּכְתִיב עַל הַבֶּגֶד, מַה בֶּגֶד הָרָאוּי לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, דְּאֵין לְךָ בֶּגֶד קָטָן שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ בֶּגֶד שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה אִם חָשַׁב עָלָיו לִכְלִי כְּמוֹת שֶׁהוּא, אַף כָּל הָרָאוּי לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, וְעַד שֶׁלֹּא הֻפְשַׁט אֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, וּמִשֶּׁהֻפְשַׁט הָוֵי רָאוּי לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה אִם חָשַׁב עָלָיו לַעֲשׂוֹת אוֹתוֹ מִכְסֶה לְמֶרְכָּבָה אוֹ לְכַסּוֹת בּוֹ אֶת הַמִּטָּה וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ קִצּוּעַ:


עד שלא הופשט אין טעון כבוס (before it is flayed, it does not require washing) – as it is written (Leviticus 7:20): “[and if any of its blood is splattered] upon a garment,” just as a piece of clothing is susceptible to receiving ritual impurity, for one does not have a small piece of clothing whose name is “clothing” that is not susceptible to receive ritual impurity if he thought of it as a utensil as it was, even all that are susceptible to receive ritual impurity, and from when it is flayed, it is susceptible of receiving ritual impurity, if he thought of it to make it a covering of the chariot or to cover with it the bed and he doesn’t require the trimming of the ends of the skin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:7)


**אַף מִשֶּׁהֻפְשַׁט.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נִתְקַן לִהְיוֹת כְּלִי:


אף משהופשט (even from when the hide is taken off) – all the while that it was not mended to be a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:8)


**אֵין טָעוּן כִּבּוּס.** דְּבָעִינַן דָּבָר הַמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה שֶׁאֵין מְחֻסָּר אֲפִלּוּ מַחֲשָׁבָה:


אין טיון כבוס – that we require something that is susceptible to ritual impurity, that is not missing even intention/thought.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:9)


**אֶלָּא מְקוֹם הַדָּם.** וְלֹא כָל הַבֶּגֶד:


אלא מקום הדם – and not the entire garment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:10)


**וְרָאוּי לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמְּחֻסָּר מַחֲשָׁבָה. וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא מְחֻסָּר מְלָאכָה. וּסְתָמָא כְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


וראוי לקבל טומאה – and even though it is lacking intention. And asl long it would not be lacking work. But the anonymous section is according to Rabbi Yehuda, and such is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:3:11)


**וְרָאוּי לְכִבּוּס.** לְמַעוֹטֵי כְּלִי עֵץ. דְּאַף עַל גַּב דִּמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה הוּא אֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְכִבּוּס, דְּבַר גְּרִידָה הוּא וְלֹא בַּר כִּבּוּס:


וראוי לכבוס – to exclude a wooden utensil. For even though it is susceptible to receive ritual impurity, it is not appropriate for washing, it is a mere scraping thing, and is not something washable.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:4:1)


**בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ.** בָּעֲזָרָה:


במקום קדוש – in the Temple courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:4:2)


**וּשְׁבִירַת כְּלִי חֶרֶס בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ.** דְּבָתַר דִּכְתִיב תְּכַבֵּס בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ, כְּתִיב וּכְלִי חֶרֶס אֲשֶׁר תְּבֻשַּׁל בּוֹ יִשָּׁבֵר, אִיתְקַשׁ שְׁבִירַת כְּלִי חֶרֶס לְכִבּוּס, מַה כִּבּוּס בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ אַף שְׁבִירַת כְּלִי חֶרֶס בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ:


ושבירת כלי חרס במקום קדשו – for after it is written (Leviticus 7:20): “you shall wash the bespattered part in the sacred precinct,” it is written (Leviticus 7:21): “An earthen vessel in which it was boiled shall be broken,” and an analogy is made between the breaking of an earthen vessel to washing: just as washing is done in a holy place, so the breaking of the earthen vessel is done in a holy place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:4:3)


**זֶה חֹמֶר.** אַכִּבּוּס קָאֵי:


זה חומר – this refers to washing.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:5:1)


**בֶּגֶד.** שֶׁנִּתַּז עָלָיו דַּם חַטָּאת וְיָצָא חוּץ לַקְּלָעִים:


בגד – that blood of a sin-offering was splattered on it and he went outside of the curtains/partitions of the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Zevachim 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Zevachim/r/11:5:2)


**נִטְמָא חוּץ לַקְּלָעִים.** לְאַחַר שֶׁיָּצָא. וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהַכְנִיס טֻמְאָה לָעֲזָרָה:
